   puḻal: ceṉṉaikku vaṭamēṟkē 9 mailil puḻal eṉṉum kirāmam uṇṭu. iṅku
   riṣapa tēvarukku (ātinātapakavāṉukku) oru kōyil uṇṭu. itaṉāl puḻal
   kirāmam paṇṭaikkālattil camaṇak kirāmamāka iruntirukkavēṇṭum.
   tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpa muṟaiyil amaintirunta intak kōyiliṉ kōpuram
   paḻutāyviṭṭapaṭiyāl, ceṉṉaiyiluḷḷa cila vaṭanāṭṭuc camaṇar ikkōyilai
   vaṭanāṭṭuc ciṟpa muṟaiyil putuppittuḷḷaṉar. ikkōyilil ippōtum
   vaḻipāṭukaḷ naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
   villivākkam: ceṅkalpaṭṭu māvaṭṭam ceṉṉaikku arukil uḷḷa irayil nilaiyam
   ivvūr. ivvūrt teruvil, vīṟṟirukkum kōlattil oru camaṇattiruvuruvam
   uṇṭu. [56]

   perunakar: (peṉṉakar) ceṅkalpaṭṭu māvaṭṭam maturāntakam tālukā,
   maturāntakattiliruntu vaṭamēṟkē 19 mailil uḷḷatu. intak kirāmattukkuk
   kiḻakkē camaṇak kōyil oṉṟu iṭintu kiṭakkiṟatu. ikkōyil kaṟkaḷaik
   koṇṭupōy ivvūrp perumāḷ kōyilaik kaṭṭa upayōkappaṭuttik koṇṭatākak

                     3. vaṭaārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam

   kaccūr: kāḷāsti jamīṉ, tiruvaḷḷūrukku vaṭakkē 12 mailil uḷḷatu. kaccūr
   mātarapākkam, iṅku oru camaṇa pasti uṇṭu. [58]
   nampākkam: kāḷāsti jamīṉ. tiruvaḷḷūr irayil nilaiyattiliruntu vaṭakkē 9
   mailil uḷḷatu. iṅku muṉpu, oru camaṇak kōyil iruntateṉṟum, piṟkālattil
   akkōyil caivak kōyilāka māṟṟappaṭṭa teṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ic caivak
   kōyilukku maṇṭīsvara cuvāmi kōyil eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. [59]
   kāvaṉūru: kuṭiyāttam tālukā, kuṭiyāttattiliruntu kiḻakkē 8 mail. intak
   kirāmattil camaṇat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. [60]
   kukainallūr: kuṭiyāttam tālukā. tiruvallam irayil nilaiyattiliruntu
   vaṭakkē 3 mail. iṅkuc camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. [61
   -----
   [58]. Top List Page 149 [59]. Top List Page 151
   [60]. Top list Page 160. [61]. Top List Page 160
   ---------
   kōḻamūr: kuṭiyāttam tālukā kuṭiyāttattiliruntu kiḻakkē 3 mail
   viriñcipuram irayil nilaiyattiliruntu teṉkiḻakku 3 mail. iṅkuc
   camaṇacciṉṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. [62]
   ceṉṉampaṭṭu: kuṭiyāttam tālukā. āmpūr irayil nilaiyattiliruntu teṉmēṟkē
   5 mail. ikkirāmattiṉ teṟkē 100 keja tūrattil oru kallil cetukkappaṭṭa
   ōr uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvuruvam muṉpu camaṇak kōyilil iruntatākak
   kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. [63]
   tirumaṇi: viriñcipuram irayil nilaiyattiliruntu kiḻakkē 4 mail. iṅkuc
   camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. [64]
   peruṅkaṅki: vālājāpēṭṭai tālukā. vālājā pēṭṭaikku vaṭakkē 9 mail. muṉpu
   itu camaṇariṉ mukkiya kirāmam. iṅku ērikkaraiyilum, kaliṅkiṉ arukilum,
   kirāmattil periya marattaṭiyilum camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. [65]
   cēvūr: āraṇi jākīr. āraṇiyiliruntu vaṭamēṟku 2 mail. iṅku oru paḻaiya
   camaṇak kōyil uṇṭu. [66]

   [62]. Top List Page 161 [63]. Top List Page 161
   [64]. Top List Page 161 [65]. Top List Page 162
   [66]. Top List Page 168
   -----------
   tirumalai: (vaikāvūr tirumalai) pōḷūr tālukā. pōḷūriliruntu vaṭakiḻakkē
   6 mail. vaṭamāti maṅkalam irayil nilaiyattiliruntu kiḻakkē 3 mail.
   iṅkuḷḷa kuṉṟiṉmēl nēmināta tīrttaṅkarariṉ tiruvuruvam pāṟaiyil
   periyatākac cetukkappaṭṭu mikak kampīramākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. 161/2
   aṭi uyaram. kuntavai jiṉālayam eṉṟu cācaṉaṅkaḷil itaṟkup paḻaiya peyar
   kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. cōḻa aracar kuṭumpattil piṟanta kuntavai eṉṉum
   ammaiyārāl itu amaikkappaṭṭatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil uḷḷa camaṇat
   tiruvuruvaṅkaḷil ivvuruvamē periyatu eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. ikkuṉṟiṉ
   aṭivārattilum iraṇṭu camaṇak kōyilkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ikkuṉṟil, iyaṟkaiyum
   ceyaṟkaiyumāka amaintuḷḷa kukaiyil cōḻar kālattu ōviyaṅkaḷ citaintu
   kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil camavacaraṇam pōṉṟa cittiram oṉṟu citaintu
   kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. piṟkālattu. ōviyaṅkaḷum iṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   pāṟaiyil cila ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷ aḻakākac cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   ‘‘jeyaṅkoṇṭa cōḻa maṇṭalattu paṅkaḷa nāṭṭu naṭuvil vakai mukaināṭṭup
   paḷḷic cantam vaikāvūrt tirumalai srīkuntavai jiṉālayam’’ eṉṟu
   kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟatu; (S.I.I. Vol. No. 98) kaṭaikkōṭūrt tirumalaip
   paravāti mallā māṇākkar ariṣṭanēmi ācāriyār, oru tirumēṉi (tiruvuruvam)
   ikkōyilil ceytu vaittatāka iṉṉoru kalveṭṭeḻuttuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. ‘poṉṉūr
   maṇṇai poṉṉāṇṭai makaḷ nallāttāḷ’ eṉpavar. ‘srīvihāra nāyakar poṉṉeyil
   nātar’ (arukar) tiruvuruvam amaittu ikkōyilukkuk koṭutta ceyti iṅkuḷḷa
   maṟṟoru ceyti kūṟukiṉṟatu. pallavavaracar tēviyār ciṇṇavaiyārum iḷaiya
   maṇimaṅkai eṉpavarum ikkōyiluṭaiya ārampa nantikku nantā viḷakkukkāka
   muṟaiyē aṟupatu kācum nilamum koṭutta ceytiyai iṉṉoru kalleḻuttuk
   kūṟukiṉṟatu. kōrāja kēcarivarmaṉ eṉṉum irācarāca tēvar kālattil,
   kuṇavīramāmuṉivar eṉṉum camaṇar nelvayalkaḷukku nīrpāyum poruṭṭu
   ‘kaṇicēkara marupoṟ cūriyaṉ kaliṅku’ eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ oru kaliṅku
   kaṭṭiṉār eṉpatai iṅku oru pāṟaiyil eḻutappaṭaṭuḷḷa ceyyuḷ
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.(S.I.I. Vol. I. No. 94) acceyyuḷ itu:
   alaipuriyum puṉaṟpoṉṉi āṟuṭaiya cōḻaṉ
       aruṇmoḻikku yāṇṭirupat toṉṟāva teṉṟum
       kalaipuriyu matinipuṇaṉ veṇkiḻāṉ kaṇicē
       karamarupoṟ cūriyaṉṟaṉ ṟirunāmattāl vāy
       nilainiṟkuṅ kaliṅkiṭṭu nimirvaikai malaikku
       nīṭūḻi irumaruṅkum nelviḷaiyak koṇṭāṉ
       kolaipuriyum paṭaiyaracar koṇṭāṭum pātaṉ
       kuṇavīra māmuṉivaṉ kuḷir vaikaik kōvē.

   viṭāl: vaṭa ārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattil uḷḷatu. iṅkuḷḷa iraṇṭu kuṉṟukaḷil
   iyaṟkaiyāyamainta iraṇṭu kukaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kukaikaḷiṉ muṉpuṟattil iraṇṭu
   maṇṭapaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. maṇṭapaṅkaḷil oṉṟaip pallava maṉṉaṉ eṉpavaṉum,
   maṟṟoṉṟai irācakēcari varmaṉ (ātittiyaṉ) eṉpavaṉum kaṭṭiyātāka iṅkuḷḷa
   kal eḻuttukkaḷ kūṟukiṟapaṭiyāl pallava aracaṉum cōḻa maṉṉaṉum ivaṟṟaik
   kaṭṭiyatākak koḷḷalām. ik kukaikaḷil paṇṭaik kālattil camaṇa muṉivar
   taṅkiyiruntaṉar. ‘kuṇakīrttipaṭārar vaḻi māṇākkiyār kaṉaka vīrak
   kurattiyāraiyum avar vaḻimāṇākkiyāraiyum’ pātukātta ceyti iṅkuḷḷa kal
   eḻuttuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivvūrukkuc ‘ciṅkapura nāṭṭuk kīḻvaḻi viṭāl mātēvi
   ārāntimaṅkalam’ eṉṟu kalveṭṭeḻuttuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. [67]
   puṉatākai: (pūṉāvati allatu tiruvattūr) vaṭa ārkkāṭu jillā ceyyāṟu
   tālukāvil āṉakkāvūrukku oru kal tolaivil irukkiṟatu. paṇṭaik kālattil
   camaṇar irunta ūr. iṅku oru camaṇak kōvilum iruntatu. oru caivar
   vaḷartta paṉaimaraṅkaḷ yāvum āṇpaṉaiyākap pōvataik kaṇṭu ivvūrc camaṇar
   ēḷaṉam ceyya, ataṉaip poṟāta caivar akkālattil āṅkuvanta
   ñāṉacampantariṭam kūṟa. avar patikam pāṭi āṇ paṉaikaḷaip peṇ paṉaiyākac
   ceytār eṉṟu periyapurāṇam kūṟukiṟatu. ivvūrilirunta camaṇak kōyil
   iṭikkappaṭṭut taraippakuti maṭṭuṅ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ik kōyiṟ kaṟkaḷai
   eṭuttukkoṇṭupōy ivvūrukkarukil uḷḷa tiruvōttūrc caivak kōyilaik
   kaṟṟaḷiyākak kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu [68] ivvūrc camaṇakkōyil
   pāḻpaṭavē, ikkōyilaic cērnta iraṇṭu camaṇat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ veḷiyē
   taraiyil kiṭappatākavum, ivvuruvaṅkaḷukkarukil uḷḷa kuṭṭaiyil ikkōyil
   cempuk katavukaḷum ēṉaiya poruḷkaḷum putaintu kiṭappatākavum
   kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. [69] ivvūr vayalil kiṭanta oru camaṇat tiruvuruvattai
   aracāṅkattār koṇṭupōyc ceyyāṟu tālukā āpīcil vaittiruppatākat
   terikiṟatu. tiruvōttūrt tala purāṇattilum ivvūril camaṇar irunta ceyti
   kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. [70]
   -------------
   [67]. S.I.I. Vol III Part iii No. 92
   [68]. Mr. Swells List of Antiquities, Vol. I Page 167
   [69]. N.A. Dt. Manual, P201, 202 and 168
   [70]. South Indian Epigraphy Report 1923-24 Page 6
   --------
   tiruvōttūr: itu muṟkālattil camaṇar celvākkup peṟṟirunta nakaram.
   tiruñāṉacampantar ivvūrukku vantapōtu iṅkuc caivacamaṇar kalakam
   ēṟpaṭṭu, camaṇar turattappaṭṭa ceytiyai ivvūrt talapurāṇamum
   periyapurāṇamum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivvūrc civaṉ kōyilil camaṇar turattappaṭṭa
   ceyti ciṟpamāka amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉpar. [71] ivvūrukku arukil uḷḷa
   puṉāvati eṉṉum kirāmattil muṉpu camaṇakkōyil iruntateṉṟum akkōyilkaḷaik
   koṇṭuvantu tiruvōttūrc civaṉkōyil kaṭṭappaṭṭateṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
   puṉāvati kirāmattiṉ veṭṭaveḷiyil iraṇṭu camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷ
   kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṟ karukil uḷḷa kuṭṭaiyil camaṇak kōyiliṉ ceppuk
   katavukaḷ mutaliyaṉa putaintukiṭakkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. [72]

   [71]. Ep. Rep. 1923, P.3 & 4 [72]. North Arcot Dt. Manual P.308

   tiruppaṉampūr: kāñcipurattukkup pattu mailil uḷḷatu. kāñcipurattil
   imacītaḷamakārācaṉ avaiyil pauttarkaḷuṭaṉ camayavātam ceytu veṟṟipeṟṟa
   akaḷaṅka ācāriyār eṉṉum camaṇacamayakuru ikkirāmattil taṅkiyiruntār.
   iṅkuḷḷa camaṇak kōyilukku muṉikiri ālayam eṉṟum peyar. itu im muṉivarai
   ñāpakappaṭuttum poruṭṭu ēṟpaṭṭa peyar eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. ivvālayattiṉ
   matiṟcuvaril ivvāciriyariṉ niṉaivukkuṟiyāka ivaruṭaiya pātaṅkaḷ
   cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaratu niṉaivukkuṟiyāka ivar tiruvaṭikaḷaik koṇṭa
   oru maṇṭapamum iṅku uṇṭu. ittiruvaṭikaḷukku mācimātattil pūcai
   naṭantuvarukiṟatu. ikkirāmattil paḻaimaiyum putumaiyumāṉa iraṇṭu
   camaṇakkōyilkaḷ uṇṭu. iṅkuc camaṇar ippōtum uḷḷaṉar. itaṟkuk karantai
   eṉṟum peyar vaḻaṅkap paṭukiṟatu. ikkarantaik kōyilil iṭintu citaintu
   pōṉa paḻaiya kōyil oṉṟirukkiṟatu ikkōyil kaṭṭiṭam camavacaraṇam pōṉṟu
   amaintuḷḷatu. ikkōyilil cācaṉaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kōpura vāyilil
   ippāṭal eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
   ‘‘kāvuvayal cūḻtaṭamum kaṉattamaṇik kōpuramum
       pāvaiyarkaḷ āṭalkaḷum paramamuṉi vācamuṭaṉ
       mēvupukaḻt tiruppaṟampai viṇṇavarkaḷ pōṟṟiceyyat
       tēvariṟai vaṉkamalac cēvaṭiyait toḻuvōmē.’’

   pūṇṭi: vaṭa ārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam vālājāp pēṭṭait tālukāvaic cārntatum
   ārkkāṭṭiliruntu āraṇi cellum vaḻiyil uḷḷatumāṉa ivvūril poṉṉivaṉa nātar
   kōyil eṉṉum oru ciṉakaram uṇṭu. ik kōyilil uḷḷa oru kalveṭṭeḻuttu
   aḻakiya āciriyappāviṉāl amaintatu. ikkōyil varalāṟṟiṉaik kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   atil, ‘ceyaṅkoṇṭa cōḻa maṇṭalantaṉṉil, payaṉpaṭucōlaip palkuṉṟak
   kōṭṭattu’ vēṇṭiya curakkum mēyūr nāṭṭup, pūṇṭi eṉpatu kāṇṭaku
   tirunakar’ eṉṟu ivvūr kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. iṅkirunta poṉṉinātar eṉṉum
   camaṇap periyār vīravīraṉ eṉṉum aracaṉukkuc ciṟappuc ceyya avaṉ
   makiḻntu eṉṉa vēṇṭumeṉṟu kēṭka avar vēṇṭukōḷiṉpaṭi ikkōyilai amaittu,
   vīravīraciṉālayam eṉṉum peyariṭṭu, kirāmaṅkaḷaiyum iṟaiyiliyākak
   koṭuttāṉ eṉṟu mēṟpaṭi āciriyappā kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   ikkōyililirunta cempu uruvaccilai, āraṇik kōyilukkuk koṇṭupōy
   vaikkappaṭṭiruppatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. inta vaṭṭārattil uḷḷa camaṇak
   kōyilkaḷil ik kōyil mikap paḻamaiyāṉatu. [73]

   vaḷḷimalai: vaṭaārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam kuṭiyāttam tālukāvil uḷḷa mēlpāṭikku
   arukil uḷḷatu vaḷḷimalai eṉṉum kirāmam ik kirāmattil uḷḷa kuṉṟu
   kaṟpāṟaikaḷāl amaintuḷḷatu. ik kuṉṟiṉ kiḻakkup pakkattil
   iyaṟkaiyāyamainta oru kukai uṇṭu. itaṉ pakkattil kaṟpāṟaiyil iraṇṭu.
   tokuti camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷ amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intac camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷiṉ
   kīḻk kaṉṉaṭa moḻiyil cila eḻuttukkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ikkal
   eḻuttukkaḷiṉāl, iṅkuḷḷa kukaiyai uṇṭākkiyavaṉ irācamallaṉ eṉṉum
   kaṅkakula aracaṉ eṉpatu teriyavarukiṉṟatu. mēl kūṟappaṭṭa camaṇa
   uruvaṅkaḷiṉ kīḻp poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kal eḻuttukkaḷil oṉṟu, ‘ajja nanti
   paṭṭārar intap piratimaiyaic ceytār’ eṉṟum, iṉṉōr eḻuttu,
   ‘srīpāṇarāyariṉ kuruvākiya pavaṇanti paṭṭārariṉ māṇavarākiya tēvacēṉa
   paṭṭārariṉ tiruvuruvam’ eṉṟum, maṟṟōr eḻuttu ‘pāla cantira paṭṭāraratu
   cīṭarākiya ajjananti paṭṭārar ceyta piratimai; kōvarttaṉa paṭṭārar
   eṉṟālum avarē’ eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
   iṅkuḷḷa camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷaikkoṇṭum kalleḻuttiṉāl aṟiyappaṭum peyarkaḷaik
   koṇṭum itu camaṇarkaḷukkuriyatu eṉpatu veḷḷiṭaimalaipōl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
   ākavē, paṇṭaik kālattil inta malaiyum intak kirāmamum camaṇarkaḷukku
   uriyatāy avarkaḷiṉ celvākkup peṟṟiruntatu eṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭum. [74]

   [74]. A Jain Rock Inscription at Vallimalai by H. Hultzsch,Ph.D.;
   Epigraphia Indica Vol. IV P.140-42. ; Top List Vol I Page 120.
   ௸ kal eḻuttiṟ kūṟappaṭum ‘pavaṇanti paṭṭārar’ eṉpavar naṉṉūl eṉṉum
   ilakkaṇa nūlai iyaṟṟiya pavaṇanti muṉivarākalām eṉpatu cilar karuttu.

   pañcapāṇṭava malai: ārkkāṭu nakarattukkut teṉ mēṟkil nāṉku mail
   tūrattil pañcapāṇṭava malai eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa kaṟpāṟaiyāṉa oru kuṉṟu
   uḷatu. ikkuṉṟukkup pañcapāṇṭava malai eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭṭa pōtilum
   uṇmaiyil pañcapāṇṭavarukkum inta malaikkum yātoru toṭarpum ilatu.
   itaṟku vaḻaṅkappaṭum iṉṉoru peyar tiruppāmalai eṉpatu. ip peyar
   tiruppāṉmalai eṉpatiṉ tiripu. inta malaiyiṉ kiḻakkup puṟattil
   amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa ceyaṟkaik kukaiyil ēḻu aṟaikaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭu tūṇkaḷuṭaṉ
   kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. intak kukaikku mēlē oru kaṟpāṟaiyil camaṇa uruvam
   oṉṟu tiyāṉattil amarntiruppatu pōṉṟu cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. teṉpuṟattil
   iyaṟkaiyāka amainta oru kukaiyum atil nīr uḷḷa ciṟu cuṉaiyum
   kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ik kukaiyiṉuḷ kaṟpāṟaiyil oru peṇ uruvam iṭatu kaiyil
   cāmarai piṭittup pīṭattil amarntiruppatu pōṉṟum itaṉ valatu pakkattil
   ōr āṇ uruvam niṉṟiruppatu pōṉṟum cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. peṇ uruvam
   amarntirukkum pīṭattiṉ muṉ puṟattil kutiraimēl ōr āḷ iruppatu pōṉṟum,
   ōr āṇ uruvamum oru peṇ uruvamum niṉṟiruppatu pōṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   ik kukaiyiṉ vāyiṟ puṟattiṉ mēl uḷḷa pāṟaiyil kīḻkkaṇṭa kal eḻuttup
   poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
   ‘‘nantip pōttaracaṟku aimpatāvatu: nākananti kuravar irukka
   poṉṉiyākkiyār paṭimam kottuvittāṉ pukaḻāḷai maṅkalattu maruttuvaṉ makaṉ
   nāraṇaṉ.’’
   eṉavē, nantippōttaracaṉ eṉṉum pallava maṉṉaṉ kālattil nākananti eṉṉum
   kuruvukkāka nāraṇaṉ eṉpavaṉ ‘poṉ iyakki’ eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa uruvattai
   amaittāṉ eṉpatu intak kal eḻuttiṉ karuttu. ik kukaiyil kāṇappaṭum
   cāmarai piṭitta peṇ uruvam itil kuṟippiṭṭa ‘poṉ iyakki’ yiṉa uruvam
   eṉṟum, ataṉ pakkattil niṟkum āṇ uruvam nākananti eṉpavariṉ uruvam eṉṟum
   karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   inta malaiyil poṟikkappaṭṭa iṉṉoru kal eḻuttum uṇṭu. atu, ki.pi. 984il
   aracāṭcikku vanta irācarāca cōḻaṉukkuk kīḻppaṭṭa lāṭarācaṉ vīra cōḻaṉ
   eṉpavaṉāl poṟikkappaṭṭatu. atil, inta malai, ‘paṭavūrk kōṭṭattup
   peruntimiri nāṭṭut tiruppāṉ malai’ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. inta lāṭarācaṉ
   vīracōḻaṉ taṉ maṉaiviyuṭaṉ inta malaiyil irunta kōyilukku vantu
   teyvattai vaṇaṅkiyapōtu avaṉ maṉaivi ik kōyilukkut tāṉam
   koṭukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu kēṭṭukkoḷḷa ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭu avvaracaṉ ‘kūṟakaṉpāṭi
   kaṟpūra vilai’ yāyum, ‘anniyāyavāla taṇṭa iṟaiyaiyum’ ik kōyilukkup
   ‘‘paḷḷic cantamākak’’ koṭuttāṉ. ik kal eḻuttiṉ kaṭaippakuti, ‘ippaḷḷic
   cantattaik kolvāṉ kaṅkaiyiṭaik kumariyiṭaic ceytār ceyta pāvaṅ koḷvār.
   itu vallatip paḷḷic cantattaik keṭuppār.................it tarmattai
   rakṣa¤ppāṉ pāta tuḷi eṉ talai mēlaṉa. aṟa maṟavaṟka. aṟamallatu
   tuṇaiyillai’ eṉṟu muṭikiṉṟatu. intac cācaṉattil kūṟappaṭṭa kūṟakaṉpāṭi
   kirāmam eṉpatu, pañca pāṇṭava malaikkuk kiḻakkē iraṇṭu mail tūrattil
   uḷḷa ‘kūṟāmpāṭi’ eṉṉum kirāmam eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
   inta malaiyil kāṇappaṭum camaṇavuruvamum iyakki uruvamum malaik
   kukaiyil uḷḷa aṟaikaḷum cācaṉattil kūṟappaṭum nākananti kuravar, paḷḷic
   cantam eṉṉum peyarkaḷum intamalai oru kālattil
   camaṇarkaḷukkuriyatāyiruntateṉpatai aṇuvaḷavum aiyamiṉṟit
   terivikkiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭaik kālattil inta malaiyil camaṇa muṉivar tavam
   purintiruntaṉar eṉpataṟku iṅkuḷḷa kukai cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟatu. [75]

   poṉṉūr: itu vaṭa ārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam vantavāci tālukāvil uḷḷa ūr. itaṟku
   aḻakiya cōḻa nallūr eṉṉum peyarum vaḻaṅkiyatākat terikiṉṟatu. ippōtuḷḷa
   camaṇarkaḷiṉ mukkiya ūrkaḷil oṉṟu. ivvūril ātinātar kōyil irukkiṉṟatu.
   ik kōyilil uḷḷa jvālāmāliṉi ammaṉ (iyakki) pērpōṉatu.- ivvūrukku 2 mail
   tūrattil uḷḷa poṉṉūr malai eṉṉum oru kuṉṟil ēlācāriyār eṉpavariṉ
   tiruppātam irukkiṉṟatu. inta ēlācāriyār eṉpavar tirukkuṟaḷ iyaṟṟiya
   tiruvaḷḷuvar eṉṟu camaṇarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. inta malaiyil pāṟaiyil
   amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa ēlācāriyariṉ tiruppātattiṟku āṇṭutōṟum camaṇarkaḷ
   pūcai mutaliya ciṟappuc ceytu varukiṟārkaḷ. poṉṉūraic cuvarṇapuri eṉṟum
   kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.
   tēcūr: vantavāci tālukā. vantavācikkut teṉ mēṟku 10 mail. iṅkuc camaṇar
   uḷḷaṉar. (Top List. P. 170., N.A. Dt. Mannual P. 215).
   teḷḷāṟu: vantavāciyiliruntu teṉmēṟku 8 mailil uḷḷatu. iṅku oru camaṇak
   kōyil uṇṭu. (Top. List. P. 170).
   tirakkōl: vantavāciyiliruntu teṉmēṟku 81/2 mail. iṅkuḷḷa kuṉṟiṉmēlē
   mūṉṟu camaṇakkōyilkaḷum mūṉṟu kukaikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. (Top. List. P. 170).

                      4. teṉārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam

   kīḻkkuppam: (kīḻaruṅkuṇam) kūṭalūr nellik kuppam cālaikaḷ cērum iṭattil
   uḷḷa ivvūril, kirāmatēvatai ammaṉ kōyiliṉ mēṟpuṟam oru camaṇar
   tiruvuruvam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ceṅkaṟ cūḷaikkāka maṇṇait tōṇṭiyapōtu itu
   kiṭaittatu. vīṟṟirukkum kōlattuṭaṉ uḷḷa inta uruvattiṉ talaikku mēl
   kuṭaiyum, iru pakkaṅkaḷilum cāmarai vīcuvatu pōṉṟa iraṇṭu uruvaṅkaḷum
   uḷḷaṉa. [76]
   --------
   [76]. South Arcot District Gazetteer, Vol. I. P.311
   --------
   tiruvatikai: teṉ ārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam kaṭalūril iruntu paṇruṭṭikkup pōkum
   cālaiyil 14 mailil uḷḷa ūr. ‘tiruvati’ eṉṟum ‘tiruvīti’ eṉṟum
   vaḻaṅkuvar. tiruvatikai vīraṭṭāṉam eṉṉum caivak kōyil iṅku uḷḷatu.
   camaṇarāka irunta taruma cēṉar, caivarāka māṟi ivvūril cūlainōy
   tīrappeṟṟut tirunāvukkaracar eṉṉum peyar peṟṟār. pāṭalipurattil
   (tiruppātirippuliyūr) irunta camaṇap paḷḷiyai iṭittu ak kaṟkaḷaik
   koṇṭuvantu ‘kuṇaparaṉ’ eṉṉum aracaṉ (makēntiravarmaṉ) ivvūril ‘kuṇatara
   vīccuram’ eṉṉum kōyilaik kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu periya purāṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu
   itaṉāl, ivvūril camaṇarum camaṇa maṭamum camaṇak kōyilum
   paṇṭaikkālattil irunta ceyti terikiṟatu.
   ivvūr vayalkaḷil iraṇṭu camaṇat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. oṉṟu
   41/2 aṭi uyaramuṭaiyatāy amarntirukkum tirukkōlattuṭaṉ uḷḷatu. iḵtu
   ivvūrccivaṉ kōyilil vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. maṟṟoṉṟu, kumarappanāyakaṉ
   pēṭṭaiyil uḷḷa cattirattil vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu 31/2 aṭi uyaram
   uḷḷatu. [77] jaṭāvarmaṉ cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉuṭaiya 13 + 13 -āvatu āṇṭil,
   nālmuka nāyaṉār muṉaiyatīccuram uṭaiya nāyaṉār kōyil nilam, arhatēvar
   (arukattēvar= camaṇak kaṭavuḷ) kōyil nilam iraṇṭiṟkum ellaiyil
   caccaravu ēṟpaṭṭa ceyti iṅkuk kiṭaitta cācaṉattiṉāl teriya varukiṟatu.
   [78] itaṉāl, iṅkuc camaṇak kōyilkaḷum akkōvilkaḷukkuriya nilaṅkaḷum
   irunta ceyti aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. iṅkuk kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa (mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa)
   iraṇṭu camaṇat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷum itaṉ uṇmaiyai uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.
   cācaṉattil kūṟappaṭukiṟa ‘nālmukanāyaṉār kōyil’ eṉpatum camaṇakkōyilāka
   irukkakkūṭum. ēṉeṉṟāl, arukak kaṭavuḷukku ‘nāṉmukaṉ’ (nāṉku aticaya
   mukaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyavar) eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.

   [77]. South Arcot District Gazetteer P. 318, Ep. Rep.1923-24. Ep.Rep
   1921-22 P.99.
   [78] . 416 of 1921. Ep.Rep. 1921-22, p.105.

   tiruppātirippuliyūr: (pāṭalipuram) tiruppāppuliyūr eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum.
   teṉ ārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattil uḷḷatu. kaṭalūr eṉpatum ituvē. itu paṇṭaik
   kālattil pāṭalipuram eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruntatu. iṅku muṟkālattil camaṇa
   maṭamum camaṇak kōyilum iruntaṉa. intap pāṭalipurattuc camaṇa maṭam
   mikap paḻamai vāyntatu. carvananti eṉṉum camaṇa muṉivar inta maṭattil
   taṅkiyiruntapōtu lōkavipākam eṉṉum nūlai arttamākati yiliruntu
   vaṭamoḻiyil moḻi peyarttār. itu, caka āṇṭu 380 il (ki.pi 458 il)
   kāñciyil aracāṇṭa cimma varmaṉ eṉṉum pallava maṉṉaṉuṭaiya 22 āvatu
   āṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭatu eṉṟu annūl kūṟukiṟatu (Mysore Archaeological Report
   1909-10. Page 45, 46) ip pāṭalipurac camaṇa maṭattil kalvikaṟṟup piṉṉar
   im maṭattiṉ talaivarāka viḷaṅkiyavar tarumacēṉar eṉpavar. ivar, piṟaku
   caiva matattil cērntu appar eṉap peyar peṟṟār. [79] iṅkirunta camaṇa
   maṭattai iṭittu akkaṟkaḷaik koṇṭupōy, tiruvatikaiyil ‘kuṇataravīccuram’
   eṉṉum kōyilaik ‘kuṇaparaṉ’ eṉṉum aracaṉ kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpar.[80] iṅkuc
   camaṇar kōyil irunta teṉpatai uṟutippaṭutta, mañcakkuppam cālaiyil
   yātrikar paṅkaḷāvukku arukil iṉṟum oru camaṇat tiruvuruvam
   kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. [81] itu 4 aṭi uyaram uḷḷatu.
   tiṇṭivaṉam: tiṇṭivaṉam tālukā. iṅkuḷḷa tōṭṭam oṉṟil mukkuṭaiyuṭaṉ
   vīṟṟirukkum camaṇavuruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ irupuṟattilum iyakkikaḷ
   cāmarai vīcuvatupōl amaintuḷḷatu. itu ceñciyil iruntu iṅkuk
   koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. [82]

   [80]. ௸, ௸ 146.
   [81]. South Arcot Dt. Gazetteer, P.369.

   ciṟukaṭampūr: tiṇṭivaṉam tālukāvil uḷḷatu. ceñcikku vaṭakkē 1 mail.
   iṅkuḷḷa ērikkaraiyiṉ mēl uḷḷa pāṟaiyil 41/2 aṭi uyaram uḷḷa camaṇat
   tiruvuruvam irukkiṟatu. iraṇṭākap piḷavupaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṟkarukil maṟṟoru
   kaṟpāṟaiyiṉmēl, niṉṟa kōlattōṭu iṉṉoru tīrttaṅkarariṉ tiru uruvamum,
   varicaiyāka 24 tīrttaṅkarariṉ tiruvuruvaṅkaḷum amaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   [83] ivai, nalla nilaiyil ippōtum puttam putitākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   [84] ippāṟaikkut tirunātar kuṉṟu eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. iṅku,
   cantirananti āciriyarum, iḷaiyapaṭārar eṉpavarum muṟaiyē 57 nāḷum 30
   nāḷum uṇṇā nōṉpiruntaṉar eṉṟu iṅkuḷḷa kalveṭṭuc cācaṉam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   [85] iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum vaṭṭeḻuttuc cācaṉam ki.pi 3 allatu 4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil
   eḻutappaṭṭateṉṟum, tamiḻnāṭṭil kāṇappaṭum vaṭṭeḻuttuc cācaṉaṅkaḷil
   ituvē mikap paḻamaiyāṉatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar.

   mēlcittāmūr: tiṇṭivaṉam tālukā. iṅkuḷḷa mallinātar kōyilil mallinātar,
   pākupali, pārcuvanātar, makāvīrar ivarkaḷiṉ tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivai
   veku aḻakāka amaintuḷḷaṉa. iṅku oru camaṇa maṭam uṇṭu. ituvē
   tamiḻnāṭṭuc camaṇamaṭam. immaṭattil ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷum uṇṭu. ceṉṉai
   mayilāppūril irunta nēminātar kōvil kaṭalil muḻukiya pōtu aṅkirunta
   nēminātar tiruvuruvattai iṅkuḷḷa kōyilil koṇṭuvantu vaittirukkiṟārkaḷ.
   [86] iṅkuḷḷa kōyilkaḷil cila cācaṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. [86(a)]
   toṇṭūr: tiṇṭivaṉam tālukā. tiṇṭivaṉattiṟku mēṟkē 6 mailil uḷḷatu.
   ceñciyil iruntu 8 mailil uḷḷatu. ivvūrukkut teṟkē oru mailil oru kuṉṟu,
   ‘pañcapāṇṭava malai’ eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ uḷḷatu. itil, iraṇṭu kukaikaḷum,
   cila kaṟpaṭukkaikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. kukaikkuḷ 2 aṭi uyaramuḷḷa tīrttaṅkarar
   tiruvuruvam oṉṟu uḷḷatu. [86(b)]
   ivvūril, vaḻuvāmoḻip perumpaḷḷi eṉṉum camaṇak kōyil iruntatākac
   cācaṉattiṉāl terikiṟatu. inta vaḻuvāmoḻip perumpaḷḷi viḷākattiṟku,
   ivvūraic cērnta kuṇaṉērimaṅkalam eṉṉum vaḻuvāmoḻi ārāntamaṅkalattaiyum
   tōṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum kiṇaṟukaḷaiyum viṇṇavakōvaraiyaṉ vairimalaiyaṉ eṉṉum
   ciṟṟaracaṉ paḷḷiccantamākat tāṉam ceytāṉ eṉṟum, intat tāṉattaip pāmpūr
   vaccira ciṅka iḷamperumāṉaṭikaḷum avarvaḻi māṇavarum mēṟpārvai pārttu
   varavēṇṭum eṉṟum iccācaṉam kūṟukiṉṟatu. [86(c)]
   viḻuppuram: viḻuppuram tālukāviṉ talainakaram. iṅku yāttirikar
   paṅkaḷāvukku arukil uḷḷa ‘paṭṭā nilam’ eṉṉum iṭattil muṉpu camaṇak
   kōyil iruntatu. ippōtu ikkōyil illai. iṅku iruntu citaintupōṉa camaṇat
   tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ (Tate Park) eṉṉum iṭattil vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉavām. [87]
   ariyāṅkuppam: putuccērikku arukil uḷḷa ūr. iṅku 4 aṭi uyaram uḷḷa
   camaṇat tiruvuruvam uḷḷatu. itaṉai ōr āṇṭi, ‘piramā’ eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ
   pūcaiceytu varukiṟār. [88] ariyāṅkuppam eṉpatu arukaṉ kuppam eṉpataṉ
   marūu pōlum.

   pāvaṇṭūr: tirukkōyilūrukkut teṉkiḻakkil 9 mailil paṇruṭṭi cālaiyil
   uḷḷatu. ivvūril, paṇṭaikkālattil camaṇarum camaṇak kōyilum
   iruntirukkavēṇṭum. ivvūril irunta riṣapatēvariṉ tiruvuruvam
   tirunaṟuṅkoṇṭai camaṇak kōyilil irukkiṟatu.
   tirunaṟuṅkoṇṭai: (tirunaṟuṅkuṉṟam - tirunaṟuṅkuṇam.) tirukkōyilūr
   tālukā. tirukkōyilūrukku, 12 mailil uḷḷatu. ivvūrukku vaṭakkē cumār 60
   aṭi uyaram uḷḷa pāṟaikkuṉṟil kōyil irukkiṟatu. malaikkuc cellap paṭikaḷ
   uṇṭu. ikkōyilil pārcuvanātar tiruvuruvam irukkiṟatu. pārcuvanātar
   kōyilai appāṇṭainātar kōyil eṉṟum kūṟuvar. niṉṟa tirumēṉi. iṅkuc
   cantiranātar kōyilum uḷḷatu. iṅkup pala cācaṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   [89] kulōttuṅkac cōḻaratu 9 āvatu āṇṭil, vīracēkar kāṭavarāyar eṉpavar
   iṅkirunta nāṟpatteṇṇāyiram perumpaḷḷikku varippaṇam tāṉam
   ceytirukkiṟār. irācarāca tēvaratu 13 āvatu āṇṭil iṅkirunta
   mēlaippaḷḷikkup paṇam tāṉam ceyyappaṭṭa ceytiyai iṉṉoru cācaṉam
   kūṟukiṟatu. intat tāṉattai ātipaṭṭārakar puṣpacēṉar eṉpavariṭam
   oppuvikkappaṭṭatu. appāṇṭārkku vaikācittirunāḷ ciṟappu
   naṭaipeṟṟataiyum, taimātattil oru tiruviḻā naṭaipeṟumpaṭi nilam tāṉam
   ceyyappaṭṭataiyum, tiripuvaṉa cakkaravartti kōṉēriṉmai koṇṭāṉ kālattuc
   cācaṉam oṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. ‘tirunaṟuṅkoṇṭai periyapāḻi āḻvārkku nilam
   tāṉam ceyyappaṭṭa ceyti iṉṉoru cācaṉam kūṟukiṉṟatu. iṅkirunta
   kīḻaippaḷḷikku srītaraṉ eṉpavar poṉ tāṉam ceytatai iṉṉoru cācaṉam
   kūṟukiṉṟatu. ikkōyil stalapurāṇam ivvūrc camaṇariṭam uṇṭu. [90]
   olakkūr: tiṇṭivaṉam tālukā, pirāmaṇa vītiyil uḷḷa citaintu tēyntupōṉa
   kalcācaṉam, ‘pirutivi viṭaṅka kuratti’ eṉṉum camaṇa āriyāṅkaṉaiyaik
   kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. [91]
   tirukkōyilūr: tirukkōvalūr eṉpatu itaṉ cariyāṉa peyar. iṅkuḷḷa perumāḷ
   kōyililuḷḷa koṭimaram camaṇaruṭaiya māṉastāmpam pōṉṟirukkiṟatu. ākaiyāl
   itu ātiyil camaṇar kōyilāka iruntirukkakkūṭum eṉṟu aiyuṟukiṉṟaṉar. [92]
   iṅku aracāṇṭa aracarkaḷil palar camaṇarāka iruntaṉar eṉpatum
   karutattakkatu.

   tātāpuram: itaṉ paḻaiya peyar irācarācapuram (tiṇṭivaṉam tālukā.)
   ivvūrp perumāḷ kōviliṉ vaṭakku, mēṟkuc cuvarkaḷil uḷḷa cācaṉaṅkaḷiṉāl
   iṅkuc camaṇak kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. iccācaṉam
   irācakēcarivar
   marāṉa irācarāca tēvaratu 21 āvatu āṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭatu. ‘kuntavai
   jiṉālayam’ eṉṉum camaṇak kōyilaip parāntaka kuntavaip pirāṭṭiyār eṉṉum
   cōḻa araciyār (ivar poṉ māḷikait tuñciya tēvaruṭaiya makaḷār.) kaṭṭi,
   akkōyilukkup poṉ, veḷḷip pāttiraṅkaḷaiyum, muttukkaḷaiyum tāṉam
   ceytatai iccācaṉam kūṟukiṉṟatu. [93] vaṭa ārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam pōḷūr tālukā
   tirumalaiyil uḷḷa camaṇak kōyilaiyum, tirucci tirumalaivāṭiyil uḷḷa
   camaṇak kōyilaiyum inta ammaiyār kaṭṭiṉār.(S.I. Vol. No. 67,68.)
   vēlūr: (tiṇṭivaṉam tālukā). iṅkirunta camaṇak kōyilai jayacēṉar eṉpavar
   paḻututīrttup putuppittār eṉṟu oru cācaṉam kūṟukiṟatu. [94]

   [93]. 8 of 1919. [94]. I 124 of 1919.

   vīracēkarap perumpaḷḷi: itu, vantavāci tālukāvil uḷḷa caḷukki eṉṉum
   ūril irunta kukaik kōyil eṉpatu cācaṉaṅkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. [95]
   perumaṇṭūr: (tiṇṭivaṉam tālukā.) iṅkuḷḷa cantiranātar kōyil
   maṇṭapattūṇil uḷḷa cācaṉam, ‘perumāṇṭai nāṭṭup perumāṇṭai iravikula
   cuntarap perumpaḷḷi’yaik kūṟukiṟatu.[96] iṉṉoru cācaṉam perumpaḷḷiyaik
   kuṟikkiṟatu. [97]
   tiṭṭaikkuṭi: (viruttācalam tālukā.) iṅkuḷḷa vaittiyanāta cuvāmi kōyil
   cācaṉaṅkaḷ, ‘makatai maṇṭalattut teṉkarait toḻuvūr paṟṟil vākaiyūr
   paḷḷiccantattaiyum’, [98] iṭaic ciṟuvāy amaṇaṉpaṭṭu [99] eṉṉum ūraiyum
   kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl, paṇṭaik kālattil iṅkuc camaṇar irunta ceyti
   aṟiyappaṭum.
   kīḻur: (tirukkōyilūr tālukā.) iṅkuḷḷa cācaṉam,
   ‘...................kaṇṇeṉak
       kāviyar kayalpayi lāviyū rataṉil
       tikkuṭai yivarum mukkuṭaiyavartam
       aṟappuṟa māṉa tiṟappaṭa nīkki’

   eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, iṅku mukkuṭaiyavarkku (arukak kaṭavuḷukku)
   uriya nilaṅkaḷ irunta ceyti aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. [100]

   paḷḷiccantal: (tirukkōyilūr tālukā.) iṅkuḷḷa oru ciṟu kuṉṟiṉmēl
   citaintupōṉa ceṉṉiyammaṉ kōyil eṉṉum camaṇak kōyil irukkiṟatu.
   pākupaliyiṉ tirumēṉiyum citaintu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṅkuḷḷa cācaṉam cakam
   1452 (ki.pi. 1530) il vijaya nakara aracar accutatēva makārāyar
   kālattil eḻutappaṭṭatu. itil, ‘jōṭivari,’ ‘cūlavari’ eṉṉum
   varippaṇattai, jampaiyilirunta nāyaṉār vijayanāyakar kōyilukkut tāṉam
   ceyyappaṭṭa ceyti kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. [101] itil kūṟappaṭukiṟa jampai
   eṉṉum ūr paḷḷiccantalukku orumailil uḷḷatu. jampaiyil uḷḷa cācaṉam
   oṉṟu, parakēcari varmaṉ (parāntakaṉ 1) uṭaiya 21 āvatu āṇṭil
   eḻutappaṭṭatu. ‘vālaiyūr nāṭṭup perumpaḷḷi’ eṉṉum camaṇak kōyilukku
   uriya ēriyaip parāmarikkumporuṭṭu nilam (ērippaṭṭi) tāṉam ceytatai itu
   kūṟukiṟatu.[102] itaṟku appāl oru mailil uḷḷa oru vayalil irācarāca
   cōḻaṉ III uṭaiya cācaṉam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itil, ‘kaṇṭarātittap
   perumpaḷḷi’ eṉṉum camaṇak kōyil kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu.[103] aṉṟiyum,
   kaṇṭarātittap perumpaḷḷiyiṉ talaivarāṉa nēminātar eṉpavariṉ uttaravai
   (kaṭṭaḷaiyai) iccācaṉam kūṟukiṉṟatu. akkaṭṭaḷai eṉṉaveṉṟāl, jampai eṉṟu
   kūṟappaṭum vīrarācēntirapurattiṉ oru pakuti ‘cōḻatuṅkaṉ āḷavantāṉ
   añciṉāṉ pukaliṭam’ eṉṉum peyar uṭaiyatu eṉpatum, ivviṭattiṟku vantu
   aṭaikkalam pukuntavaraik kāttu ātarikkavēṇṭum eṉpatum ākum.

   [101]. 449 of 1937-38. Ep. Rep. 1937-38 P. 104.
   [102]. 446 of 1937-38.
   [103]. 448 of 1937-38. Ep. Rep. 1937-38. P.89.

   (kuṟippu: ‘añciṉāṉ pukaliṭam’ eṉpatu aṭaikkala tāṉattaik kuṟikkum.
   camaṇar akāratāṉam, oḷaṭatatāṉam, cāstiratāṉam, aṭaikkalatāṉam, eṉṉum
   innāṉku tāṉaṅkaḷaic ciṟappākak koḷvar. intat tāṉaṅkaḷaip paṟṟic camaṇa
   nūlkaḷil virivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṭaikkala tāṉattaittāṉ añciṉāṉ
   pukaliṭam eṉṟu intac cācaṉam kūṟukiṉṟatu.)
   cōḻavāṇṭipuram: (tirukkōyilūr tālukā.) iṅkē kīraṉūr eṉṉum kirāmattil
   ‘pañcaṉām pāṟai’ eṉappaṭum kaṟpāṟaikaḷil kōmaṭīsvarar, pārcuvanātar
   tiruvuruvaṅkaḷum kaṟpaṭukkaikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. iṅkup paṇṭaik kālattil
   camaṇar ciṟappuṟṟiruntaṉar.[104] cōḻa vāṇṭipurattil uḷḷa ‘āṇṭi
   malaiyil’ 10 ām nūṟṟāṇṭuk kalveṭṭukkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vēli
   koṅkaraiyar puttaṭikaḷ eṉpavar iṅkuḷḷa. ‘tēvārattai’ (kōyilai)
   amaittatāka ivai kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. malaip pāṟaiyil, patmāvati ammaṉ,
   kōmaṭīsvarar, pārcuvanātar, makāvīrar tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   patmāvati ammaṉaik kāḷiyammaṉ eṉṟu ivvūrār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. [105] iṅkuḷḷa
   maṟṟoru cācaṉam, cittavaṭavaṉ eṉpavar pāṇappāṭi eṉṉum ūrai iṅkuḷḷa
   piṇṭik kaṭavuḷukkum (arukakkaṭavuḷukkum), mātavarukkum tāṉam ceytataik
   kūṟukiṉṟatu. tāṉam koṭukkap paṭṭa ivvūraik kurantikuṇavīrapaṭārarum
   avar vaḻimāṇākkarum mēṟpārvai pārttuvantatāka iccācaṉam
   kūṟukiṟatu.[106]

   itil, tāṉam ceyta cittavaṭavaṉ eṉpavar vēli koṅkarāyar puttaṭikaḷ eṉṟu
   peyar peṟuvār. kōval nāṭṭaracaṉāṉa cittavaṭavaṉ eṉṉum cēti aracaṉum
   malaiya kulōtpavaṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭupavaṉum ivarē eṉpar. ivarē, vēḷir
   koṅkarāyar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟār. aṉṟiyum ivar, cētināṭṭu ōri
   kuṭumpattavar eṉṟum, pāri kuṭumpattil peṇ koṇṭavar eṉṟum
   kūṟappaṭukiṟār. (ōri, pāri eṉṉum kuṭumpattaic cērnta ciṟṟaracar caṅka
   nūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ kaṭai ēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷaic cērntavar.)
   irāṣṭrakūṭa aracaṉ kaṉṉara tēvar kālattil (10 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil),
   ivvaracaṉukkuk kīḻppaṭṭut tirukkōvalūrai aracāṇṭa malāṭa aracaṉ
   cattinātaṉ eṉṟum cittavaṭavaṉ eṉṟum peyaruḷḷa naracimmavarmaṉtāṉ ivaṉ
   eṉṟum cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. [107]
   ivvūrukku arukil uḷḷa tēviyakaram, elantuṟai eṉṉum ūrkaḷilum
   pārcuvanātar mutaliya camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. intat teṉ
   ārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattil iṉṉum pala iṭaṅkaḷil camaṇarkaḷum camaṇak
   kōyilkaḷum irunta ceytikaḷ cācaṉaṅkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟaic
   curukkamākak kūṟuvōm:
   tirukkōyilūr tiruvīraṭṭāṉamuṭaiya tēvarukkuriya nilam tāṉam ceyyappaṭṭa
   cācaṉattil, ‘paḷḷic cantamākiya iyakkippaṭṭi’ kuṟikkappaṭukiṟatu.[108]
   (itil, iyakkippaṭṭi eṉpatu iyakkiyākiya yakṣa¤kkut tāṉam ceyyappaṭṭa
   nilattaik kuṟikkum. iyakki, yakṣa¤ eṉpatu camaṇa tīrttaṅkarariṉ
   parivāra teyvaṅkaḷil oṉṟu.)
   tirukkōvalūr akattīsvarar kōvil nilattaik kuṟikkum cācaṉam oṉṟu,
   ‘kuḷavi kuḻi paḷḷic cantam’ eṉṉum nilattaik kuṟikkiṟatu. [109]
   citamparattuc cācaṉaṅkaḷ, ‘tiruvampalap perumpaḷḷi nallūr’ eṉṉum
   ūraiyum, [110] 'āṟṟūrāṉa rājarāja nallūril’ irunta ‘paḷḷic canta’
   nilaṅkaḷaiyum,[111] kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. tiruvaṇṇā malaic cācaṉam,
   ‘maturāntaka vaḷanāṭṭu āṭaiyūrnāṭṭu āṭaiyūr’ vaṭakkil ēri
   kīḻppārkkellai paḷḷic cantam mēl pārk kellai paḷḷic cantam nilaṅkaḷai’k
   kuṟikkiṉṟatu.[112] iṉṉoru cācaṉam, ‘taccūr paḷḷic canta’ttaik
   kuṟikkiṟatu.[113] ivaṟṟiliruntu iṅkellām camaṇak kōyilukkuriya nilaṅkaḷ
   iruntaṉa eṉpatu pulaṉākum.
   koliyaṉūr: (kōylaṉūr eṉa vaḻaṅkum) viḻuppuram tālukāvil
   viḻuppurattiṟkut teṉkiḻakkē 4 mailil uḷḷatu. kilamāyppōṉa camaṇak kōyil
   iṅku uṇṭu. iṅkuc cācaṉaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.[114] kōliyapuranallūr
   eṉpatu itaṉ paḻaiya peyar.[115] ‘svasti srī nayiṉār tēvar perumāṉār srī
   kōyil tiruviruppuk kalpaṇi iṭaiyāṟaṉ tirumaṟumārpaṉ vaṇikapurantaraṉ
   tiruppaṇi’ eṉṟu oru cācaṉam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu,[116] ‘‘kāḷayuktiu āṉimī
   10u srīmaṉ makāmaṇṭalēsvara āruva acuranārāyaṇa tiyāka camuttira immaṭi
   torāta vacavaiya tēva makārācāviṉ kāriyattukkuk karttarāṉa nalla tampi
   mutaliyār periya tampiyār koliyāpura nallūr nayiṉār arumoḻi nāyakar
   kōyil pūcait tiruppaṇikkup pūruvamāka vaṭakku vācalil mēṟku uḷḷa
   vicayarācapurattu ellaikku ippāl uḷḷa nañcai puñcai nāṟpāṟkellaiyum
   taṭaviṭṭuc cantirātitta varaiyum naṭatta cīmai pala paṭṭaṭaiyum
   kalveṭṭik kuṭutta taṉmattukku akutam niṉaittavaṉ keṅkaik karaiyil kārām
   pacuvaik koṉṟa pāvattilum pirāmaṇaraik koṉṟa pāvattilum
   pōkakkaṭavaṉ,’[117] eṉṟu oru cācaṉam kūṟukiṟatu.
   jiṉacintāmaṇi nallūr: viruttācalam tālukā. ivvūrp peyarē itu oru camaṇa
   ūr eṉpataic terivikkiṟatu.
   vēṭūr: viḻuppurattiṟkuk kiḻakkē 11 mailil uḷḷatu iṅkuḷḷa camaṇak kōyil
   ippōtum pūcikkappaṭukiṟatu.[118]
   eḷḷāṉācūr: tirukkōyilūr tālukā. tirukkōyilūrukkut teṟkē 161/2 mailil
   uḷḷatu. oru paḻaiya camaṇar kōyil iṅku uḷḷatu.[119]
   ceñci: ceñcik kōṭṭaikku arukil 24 tīrttaṅkararkaḷiṉ tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ
   pāṟaiyil cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (Annual Report of Arch Dept. Southern
   Circle Madras. 1912-13. P.7)

                    5. tiruccirāppaḷḷi māvaṭṭam

   uṟaiyūr: itaṉai ‘uṟantai’, ‘kōḻiyūr’ eṉṟum kūṟuvar. itu cōḻa aracariṉ
   talai nakaramāka iruntatu. ivvūril arukakkaṭavuḷiṉ kōyilum camaṇa
   muṉivarkaḷum iruntaṉar eṉṟum, kōvalaṉ kaṇṇakiyaruṭaṉ maturaikkuc ceṉṟa
   kavuntiyaṭikaḷ eṉṉum camaṇat tuṟavi, ivvūril taṅki arukakkaṭavuḷaiyum
   muṉivaraiyum vaṇaṅkiṉār eṉṟum cilappatikāram kūṟukiṉṟatu [120]. itaṉāl,
   ki.pi.2 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iṅkuc camaṇar irunta ceyti aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu.
   nīlakēci eṉṉum nūlilum, ivvūril camaṇak kōyil irunta ceyti
   kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu [121]. uṟaiyūrilum ataṉaic cārnta ūrkaḷilum paṇṭaik
   kālattil camaṇar iruntaṉar. ivvūraic camaṇar tamatu mantira
   valimaiyiṉāl aḻittuviṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟu caiva camaya nūlākiya
   takkayākapparaṇi uraiyil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu[122]. cōḻa aracaṉmēl ciṉaṅ
   koṇṭa civperumāṉ, maṇ maḻai poḻiyac ceytu uṟaiyūrai aḻittār eṉṟu
   piṟkālattil eḻutappaṭṭa cevvantip purāṇam eṉṉum maṟṟoru caiva nūl
   kūṟukiṟatu[123]. itaṉai aḻittatu camaṇar āyiṉum ākuka; caivar āyiṉum
   ākuka; ivvūr piṟkālattil maṇmāriyāl aḻikkappaṭṭateṉpatu terikiṟatu.
   ivvūr aḻintapiṟaku, tiruccirāpaḷḷi cōḻariṉ talainakarāyiṟṟu eṉpar.
   veḷḷaṉūr: tirucci māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa inta ūriṉ vayalil kalliṉāl
   amaikkappaṭṭa camaṇat tiruvuruvac cilaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; (Arch.
   Rep. 1909-1910).
   paḻanākappaḷḷi: karūr tālukā nākampaḷḷi kirāmattil uḷḷa makāpalīsvarar
   kōvil maṇṭapattil uḷḷa cācaṉam tiripuvaṉa cakkaravartti kulōttuṅkac
   cōḻa vēntar kālattil eḻutappaṭṭatu. iccācaṉattil, paḻanākappaḷḷik
   kōyilukkut tiruviḷakkut tāṉam ceyyappaṭṭa ceyti kūṟappaṭukiṟatu[124].
   itil kuṟikkappaṭṭa paḻanākappaḷḷi eṉpatu camaṇak kōyil eṉpatil
   aiyamillai. itaṉāl iṅkup paṇṭaik kālattil camaṇar iruntatu
   aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu.
   amutamoḻip perumpaḷḷi: tirucci tālukā aṉpil eṉṉum ūril uḷḷa cācaṉam,
   tiripuvaṉa cakkaravartti irācarācacōḻa tēvaratu 19 āvatu āṇṭil
   eḻutappaṭṭatu. itil, ‘tiruviṭaikkuṭi amutu moḻip perumpaḷḷi’ eṉṉum
   camaṇak kōvil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭaḷḷatu[125].
   pulivallam: tirucci tālukā tiruppālait tuṟai tārukavaṉēsvarar kōyilil
   uḷḷa cācaṉaṅkaḷil, ‘pulivallattu ūriṭaip paḷḷiccantam’
   kūṟappaṭukiṟatu[126]. itaṉāl, camaṇak kōyilukkuriya nilaṅkaḷ
   iṅkiruntatu aṟiyappaṭum.
   amaṇkuṭi: tirucci tālukā tiruccentuṟaiyil uḷḷa cantiracēkarar kōyil
   cācaṉam, maturai koṇṭa kōppara kēcarivaṉmaratu 16 āvatu āṇṭil
   eḻutappaṭṭatu. itil, amaṇkuṭi kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu[127]. ṣa aracaṉatu
   23-āvatu āṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa cācaṉam ‘uṟaiyūr kūṟṟattu amaṇkuṭi’ yaik
   kūṟukiṉṟatu[128]. ippeyariṉāl iṅkuc camaṇar iruntatu aṟiyappaṭum.
   amaṇar eṉiṉum camaṇar eṉiṉum oṉṟē.
   -----------
   [120]. maturaik kāṇṭam, kāṭukāṇ kātai, (1-9)
   [121]. ajīvaka vātac carukkam 8
   [122]. kōyilaip pāṭiyatu. tāḻicai 70 urai
   [123]. uṟaiyūr aḻitta carukkam [124]. 258 of 1930-31
   [125]. S.I.I. (Texts) Vol. VII No.198 P.98
   [126]. S.I.I. (Texts) Vol. VIII. No. 557 and 572
   [127]. S.I.I. (Texts) Vol. VIII. No. 609
   [128]. do do No. 632

   paḻaiya caṅkaṭam: kuḷittalai tālukāvil uḷḷa ip paḻaiya caṅkaṭam,
   makātāṉapurattiṉ oru pakuti. iṅkuc camaṇac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ
   kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa[129]. iṅkup paṇṭaikkālattil camaṇar iruntaṉar.
   cīyālam: kuḷittalai tālukā cīyālattil ‘cuṇṭakkāpāṟai’ eṉṉum kuṉṟil
   camaṇa muṉivar iruntataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.[130]
   kuttālam: teṉkāci tālukāvil uḷḷa kuttālam eṉṉum iṭattil ‘paratēcip
   poṭavu’ eṉṉum kuṉṟum kukaiyum uḷḷaṉa. iṅkum paṇṭaikkālattil camaṇa
   muṉivar iruntaṉar eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa[131].
   vīrappaṭṭi: tirucciyil iruntu putukkōṭṭaikkup pōkiṟa pātaiyiṉ
   iṭatupuṟattil uḷḷa inta ūrukkarukil aṉṉavācal eṉṉum iṭattil oru vayalil
   camaṇa tīrttaṅkarariṉ tiruvuruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu[132].
   jampukēsvaram: tirucci tālukāvil uḷḷatu. ivvūr jampukēsvarar kōyilil
   uḷḷa, irāca kēcarivarmarāṉa tiripuvaṉa cakkaravartti irācarāca tēvaratu
   16 āvatu āṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa cācaṉattil, ‘kavirājap perumpaḷḷi,’ eṉṉum
   camaṇak kōyil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu[133].
   tirumalaivāṭi: iṅkuk kuntavaip pirāṭṭiyār, oru camaṇak kōyilaik
   kaṭṭiṉār eṉṟu terikiṟatu. (S.I.I. Vol.I.67&68) inta araciyār
   vaṭaārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam pōḷūrilum, teṉ ārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattilum camaṇak
   kōyilkaḷaik kaṭṭiyuḷḷār.
   periyammā pāḷaiyam: perampalūr tālukā perampalūrukku vaṭakiḻakkē 14
   mail. ik kirāmattiṉ arukil periya cālai veḷḷāṟṟaik kaṭakkiṟa iṭattil
   oru camaṇat tiruvuruvam āṟṟaṅkarai maṇaliṉāl mūṭuṇṭu kiṭakkiṟatu. iv
   vuruvattiṉ talaiyum tōḷkaḷum veḷiyē terikiṉṟaṉa.[134].
   ampāpuram: ik kirāmattukku vikramam eṉṉum peyarum uṇṭu. uṭaiyār
   pāḷaiyam tālukāvil uṭaiyār pāḷaiyattiliruntu teṉmēṟkil 11 mailil
   uḷḷatu. iṅkuc cila camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. [135].
   jayaṅkoṇṭa cōḻapuram: uṭaiyār pāḷaiyam tālukā uṭaiyār pāḷaiyattiliruntu
   vaṭamēṟkē 5 mailil uḷḷatu. iṅku ērikkaraiyil oṉṟum, oru teruvil oṉṟum
   āka iraṇṭu camaṇat tiruvuruṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ērikkaraiyilirukkiṟa tiruvurut
   tiṟku in nakara makkaḷ āṇṭukku orumuṟai pūcai ceykiṟārkaḷ. [136].
   vaṇṇam: uṇṇam eṉṟum kūṟuvar. uṭaiyār pāḷaiyattukkut teṉmēṟkil 19 mailil
   uḷḷatu. kīḻppaḷūrukkut teṟkil 2 mailil uḷḷatu. iṅku oru camaṇat
   tiruvuruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu[137].
   lālkuṭi: tirucci tālukā tiruccirāppaḷḷikku vaṭakiḻakkē 11 mail.
   ivvūrukkarukil, puḷḷampāṭikkup pōkiṟa cālaiyil iṭatu pakkattil oru
   vayaliyē oru camaṇat tiruvuruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu [138].
   makātāṉapuram: kuḷittalai tālukā kuḷittalaikku mēṟkē 13 mailil uḷḷatu.
   itu kaṅkaikoṇṭa cōḻapurattiṉ (itaṟkup paḻaiya ceṅkaṭam eṉṟum peyar.)
   oru pakutiyāka uḷḷatu. ivviṭattil pala camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷ
   kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. [139].
   civāyam: kuḷittalai tālukā kuḷittalaikkut teṟkē 5 mail. iṅku oru camaṇa
   uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu [140].
   cuṇṭaikkāppāṟai: kuḷittalaikkut teṟkē 3 mail. ikkirāmattil oru pāṟaiyiṉ
   mēl oru camaṇat tiruvuruvam amaikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu [141].
   veṭṭuvāntalai: kuḷittalaikku vaṭamēṟkē 9 mail. iṅkē mūṉṟu camaṇat
   tirumēṉikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa [142].

                             6. putukkōṭṭai

   ammā cattiram: ammā cattirattukku mēṟkē paḷḷikkuḷam eṉṉum oru kuḷam
   uṇṭu. paḷḷikkuḷam eṉṟāl, camaṇappaḷḷikkuriya kuḷam eṉpatu poruḷ. ik
   kuḷattiṟku mēṟkē 25 aṭi uyaramuḷḷa kaṟpāṟai mītu arukak kaṭavuḷiṉ
   tiruvuruvam mukkuṭaiyuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṅku iraṇṭu kalveṭṭuc
   cācaṉakaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiliruntu ik kaṟpāṟaikkut tiruppaḷḷimalai eṉṉum
   peyar uṇṭeṉṟu terikiṟatu. ittiruppaḷḷi malaikkuriya kuḷantāṉ mēṟkūṟiya
   paḷḷikkuḷam. ip paḷḷikkuḷattukkarukil vēṟu cila camaṇat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ
   citaintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   āḷuruṭṭi malai: ammā cattirattukku arukil uḷḷatu iṅkuḷḷa kuṉṟiṉmēl
   iraṇṭu camaṇat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. im malaiyiṉ kuṭakukku
   muṉpākac cācaṉam poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ic cācaṉattiṉāl, im malaikkut
   tirumāṉ malai eṉṉum peyar uṇṭeṉpatu aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. ‘cakkaravarttikaḷ
   cuntarapāṇṭiya tēvarkku..........kulōttuṅka cōḻa paṭṭaṇattu paḷḷic
   canta.......uṭaiyār kaṉakacantira paṇṭitar māṇākkar taṉmatēva ācāriyār
   pāricai........periyapaḷḷi vayalil nāyaṉār tirumāṉmalai yāḻvār paḷḷic
   cantamāy eṅkaḷukku arccaṉā pōkamāy varukiṟa nilam iraṇṭumā,’ eṉpatu ic
   cācaṉattiṉ vācakam[143]. citaintuḷḷa vēṟu camaṇat tiruvuruvaṅ kaḷum
   iṅku uḷḷaṉa.
   nārattamalai: ip peyar nakarattumalai eṉpataṉ tiripu. iraṭṭaipāṭi koṇṭa
   kulōttuṅka cōḻa nakarattu malai eṉṟu paḻaiya cācaṉam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   parakēcari varmaṉ tiripuvaṉa cakkaravartti srī kulōttuṅka cōḻa tēvar
   kālattut tirumalaik kaṭampar kōyil cācaṉam iṅkuḷḷa camaṇak kaṭavuḷait
   tirumāṉmalai arukat tēvar eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. [144]. inta malaiyiṉ oru
   pakutikkut tiruppaḷḷi malai eṉṟum maṟṟoru pakutikkut teṉtiruppaḷḷi
   malai eṉṟum peyar vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. tiruppaḷḷi malaiyil periya camaṇa
   maṭamum kōyilum iruntaṉa. im maṭaṅkaḷukkuriya nilaṅkaḷiliruntu
   kiṭaikkum varuvāyil periya maṭattukku iraṇṭu paṅkum ciṟiya maṭattukku
   oru paṅkum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. icceytikaḷ pommaippāṟaiyiṉ mēṟpurattil uḷḷa
   cācaṉattiṉāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. intac cācaṉam cakam 675 il (ki.pi. 753
   il) eḻutappaṭṭatu. atu varumāṟu:
   ”svasti srī tiripuvaṉa cakkaravartti kōṉēriṉmai koṇṭāṉ tiruppaḷḷimalaip
   paḷḷi uṭaiyārkaḷukkum teṉ tiruppaḷḷimalai uṭaiyārkaḷukkum tiruppaḷḷi
   malai nāyakarkkum tiruppaṭi māṟṟuḷḷiṭṭa nitta nipantaṅkaḷukkut teṉ
   ciṟuvāyil nāṭṭuk koṟṟamaṅkalam nāṉkellaik kuṭpaṭṭa nīr nilamum nañcey
   puṉceyyum antarāyamum tōṭṭamuṅ kuḷamum taruvatāṉa accum kāriya
   vārāṭciyum veṭṭipāṭṭamum pañcupili cantivikkiraṇap pēṟu vācaṟ pēṟu
   ilāñciṉaippēṟu taṟiyiṟai cekkiṟait taṭṭolip pāṭṭamum iṭaiyavar variyum
   iṉa variyum poṉ variyum maṟṟumep peyarp paṭṭaṉavum uṭpaṭa āṟāvatu mutal
   paḷḷiccanta iṟaiyiliyākat tiruppaḷḷimalaiyāḻvārkku irukūṟum teṉ
   tiruppaḷḷimalai nāyakarkku orukūṟum kuṭuttōm. ippaṭikku iv vōlai
   piṭipāṭāk koṇṭu puravilum variyilum kaḻippittuc cantirātittavaṟ
   celvatāka. iraṇṭu tirumalaiyilum kallilum veṭṭi nāṉkellaiyilum srī
   mukkuṭaik kallum nāṭṭikkoḷka - ivai paḻantiparāya ṉeḻuttu - āṇṭu 6075.
   ivai vilvavarāyaṉeḻuttu - ivai teṉṉavataraiyaṉeḻuttu. [145].
   paḷḷivayal: nārttalai tirumayak kaṭampar kōyilukku vaṭapuṟattup
   pātaiyil uḷḷa cācaṉam, srī kulōttuṅka cōḻa tēvaratu 27 āvatu āṇṭil
   eḻutap paṭṭatu. itil, ‘iraṭṭaipāṭi koṇṭa cōḻavaḷa nāṭṭut teluṅka
   kulakālapurattup paḷḷivayal’ nilam kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṉṟiyum, ‘ivvūrt
   tirumāṉaimalai arukattēvaṟkup puṟakarai nilam iraṇṭumā’ eṉṟum
   kūṟukiṉṟatu. [146] iṉṉoru cācaṉam, vīrappiratāpa tēvarāya makārāyar
   viceyarāyar kumārar tēvarāya makārāyar cakāptam 1353 iṉ mēl cellāniṉṟa
   irāṭcaca varuṭam (ki.pi. 1431) eḻutappaṭṭatu. ic cācaṉattilum,
   ‘kaṭalaṭaiyā tilaṅkaikoṇṭa cōḻavaḷanāṭṭu nakaram teliṅkakulakāla
   puramāṉa kulōttuṅka cōḻa paṭṭaṇattu uṭaiyār tirumalaikkaṭampūruṭaiya
   nayiṉār’ paḷḷivayalnilam iraṇṭumā’ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. [147] ivaṟṟāl iṅkup
   paṇṭaik kālattil arukakkaṭavuḷukkuriya nilaṅkaḷ iruntatu aṟiyap
   paṭukiṟatu.
   camaṇartiṭal: itaṟkuc camaṇar kuṇṭu eṉṟum vēṟu peyar uṇṭu.
   kāyāppaṭṭiyil uḷḷa veṇṇāvikkuḷattiṉ puṟakaraiyil uḷḷatu. iṅkuḷḷa kal
   oṉṟil, ‘svastisrī. tiruveṇṇāyil aiññūṟṟuvap perumpaḷḷit tiruvāyttal
   māṭam cayavīrap pēriḷamaiyāṉ’ eṉṟu poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl,
   aiññūṟṟuvap perumpaḷḷi eṉṉum camaṇa maṭamum kōyilum iṅku
   iruntirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
   caṭaiyāpāṟai: itu caṭaiyārmalai eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. tirukkōkarṇattiṟku
   arukil uḷḷa oru pāṟai itu. iṅkuc camaṇa tīrttaṅkarar tiruvuruvam oṉṟu
   uḷḷatu. ivvuruvattiṉ arukil oru cācaṉam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.[148] ic
   cācaṉattiliruntu, perunaṟkiḷḷi cōḻapperumpaḷḷi eṉṉum camaṇakkōyil iṅku
   irunta ceyti aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. ‘‘kōṉēriṉmai koṇṭāṉ teṉkavi nāṭṭāṟkut
   taṅkaḷ nāṭṭuk kallāṟṟup paḷḷip perunaṟkiḷḷi cōḻap perumpaḷḷiyāḻvāṟku
   tiruppaṭi māṟṟuḷḷiṭṭu vēṇṭum nimantaṅkaḷukku ivvūrppaḷḷi uṭaiyārkaḷ
   kāṇiyāṉa nilam mukkāl kuṭuttōm. innāṭṭuc caṭaiyārmalaimēl teṉkavi
   nāṭṭup perumpaḷḷi āḻvāṟku ivvūr.............’’ eṉṟu itil
   eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
   tēṉimalai: itaṟkut tēṉūrmalai eṉṟum peyar uṇṭu. iṅkuc cila camaṇat
   tirumēṉikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. immalaiyil malaiyattuvajaṉ eṉṉum camaṇat
   tuṟavi tavam ceyvataik kaṇṭu irukkuvēḷ eṉṉum koṭumpāḷūrc ciṟṟaracaṉ
   nilam tāṉam ceyta ceytiyai iṅkuḷḷa cācaṉam kūṟukiṉṟatu.[149] atu
   kīḻvarumāṟu:- ‘svastisrī malayattuvajaṉ tēṉūr malaiyil tavañ ceyyak
   kaṇṭu irukkuvēḷ cantittu avippuṟañ ceyta paḷḷiccantam nālēkāl.
   ivvaṟaṅkāttāṉ aṭi nīḷeṉ ceṉṉiyaṉa.’
   iṅkuḷḷa tīrttaṅkarar tiruvuruvam oṉṟiṉkīḻ, ‘svastisrī. srīvalla utaṉa
   ceruvoṭṭi ceyvitta tirumēṉi’ eṉṟu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu.[150]
   malaiyakōyil: iṅkuḷḷa iṭatupuṟap pāṟaiyil uḷḷa oru cācaṉam kuṇacēṉar
   eṉṉum camaṇap periyāraik kuṟikkiṟatu.[151] kaṟpāṟaiyil
   kuṭaintamaikkappaṭṭa iraṇṭu kōyilkaḷ iṅku uḷḷaṉa.

   cittaṉṉavācal: cittaṉṉavācal kirāmattiṟkuk kiḻakkē oru mailil vaṭakkut
   teṟkāka oru malai uḷḷatu. immalaimēl paṇṭaikkālattil camaṇa muṉivarkaḷ
   tavañceytuvantaṉar. immalaiyil kaṟpāṟaiyil kuṭaintamaikkappaṭṭa
   kukaikkōyil uṇṭu. ikkōyiliṉ muṉ maṇṭapam vaṭakkut teṟkāka 22 aṭi 10
   aṅkula nīḷamum 111/2 aṭi akalamum uḷḷatu. muṉpuṟattil iraṇṭu
   kaṟṟūṇkaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. tūṇkaḷ ovvoṉṟum 2 aṭi 2 aṅkulam caturamum 6 aṭi
   10 aṅkulam uyaramum uḷḷaṉa. ivai yāvum orē pāṟaiyaik kuṭaintu
   amaikkappaṭṭaṉa. im maṇṭapattiṉ vaṭapuṟattiṉ cuvaraiyoṭṭi,
   mukkuṭaiyuṭaṉ vīṟṟirukkum arukakkaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvuruvamum teṉpuṟac
   cuvarai yoṭṭip pārcavanātar tiruvuruvamum aḻakāka amaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   maṇṭapattiṉ mēṟkūraiyilum tūṇkaḷilum cuvarkaḷilum ōviyaṅkaḷ
   eḻutappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivvōviyaṅkaḷ ippōtu citaintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   citainta nilaiyilum ivvōviyaṅkaḷ maṉataik kavarakkūṭiyaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa.
   inta maṇṭapattukku naṭuvil pāṟaiyaik kuṭaintamaikkappeṟṟa oru ciṟu
   kōvil uṇṭu. itaṉ akalamum nīḷamum uyaramum 101/2 aṭi. ikkōyiliṉ vāyil 5
   aṭi 7 aṅkulam uyaramum 21/2 aṭi akalamum uḷḷatu. ikkōyilil
   arukakkaṭavuḷiṉ mūṉṟu uruvaṅkaḷ mukkuṭaiyuṭaṉ amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ik
   kukaik kōyilai amaittavaṉ pērpōṉa makēntiravarmaṉ I eṉṉum pallava
   aracaṉākum. ivaṉ ki.pi. 600 mutal 630 varaiyil toṇṭaimaṇṭalam
   cōḻamaṇṭalaṅkaḷai aracāṇṭāṉ. ivaṉuṭaiya uruvam ikkukaik kōyilil
   ōviyamāka amaikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ikkukaikkōyilukku vaṭakīḻppuṟattil
   iyaṟkaiyā yamainta oru kukai uḷatu. ikkukaikkuḷ 17 kaṟpaṭukkaikaḷ
   pāṟaiyil amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ikkukaikku ēḻaṭippaṭṭam eṉṉum vaḻiyākac
   cellavēṇṭum.152 ikkukaiyil muṟkālattil camaṇat tuṟavikaḷ taṅkiyiruntu
   tavañceytu vantaṉar. iṅku, pirāmi eḻuttilum vaṭṭeḻuttilum cācaṉaṅkaḷ
   uḷḷaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa cācaṉam, ‘toḻakkuṉṟattuk kaṭavuḷaṉ nīlaṉ. tiruppūraṇaṉ
   tiṭṭaiccaraṇaṉ. tiruccāttaṉ. srīpurāṇacantiraṉ, niyattakkaraṉ
   paṭṭakkāḻi.........ttūrk kaṭavuḷaṉ’ eṉṉum peyarkaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟatu.[153]
   ippeyarkaḷ iṅkut tavañceytirunta camaṇamuṉivarkaḷil cilaruṭaiya
   peyarkaḷ eṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.
   iṉṉoru cācaṉam ki.pi 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil ‘avaṉicēkaraṉ
   srīvaḷḷuvaṉ (srīvallapaṉ)’ kālattil, maturaiyāciriyaṉ iḷaṅkautamaṉ
   eṉpavar iṅkuḷḷa uḷmaṇṭapattaip paḻututīrttu veḷimaṇṭapam oṉṟaik
   kaṭṭiṉār eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.[154] maturai āciriyaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṭaimaiyāl
   ivar ciṟanta pulavarāka irukkavēṇṭum.
   iṅkuḷḷa oru tōṭṭattil uṭaipaṭṭa oru camaṇa tīrttaṅkarar tiruvuruvam
   uṇṭeṉṟum itaṉait taṭṭiṉāl, icaiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ōcai uṇṭākiṟatu eṉṟum
   kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
   ālaṅkuṭit tālukā tiruvaraṅkuḷam eṉṉum iṭattil aritīrttīsvarar kōvilil
   uḷḷa cācaṉam ki.pi. 1260 il tirupuvaṉa cakkaravartti vīrapāṇṭiyatēvar
   kālattil eḻutappaṭṭatu. itil, paḷḷiccantanilam kuṟikkappaṭukiṟatu.[155]
   it tālukā kōkarṇam kōkarṇīsvararkōvil cācaṉam vīrapāṇṭiya tēvaratu 14
   āvatu āṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭatu. itil, teṉkavināṭṭup paḷḷic canta nilaṅkaḷ
   kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.[156] it tālukā vaḷavampaṭṭi aracāṅkap pāṭacālaikku
   arukil camaṇat tiruvuruvam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. it tālukā puttāmpūril
   moṭṭaippiḷḷaiyār eṉa vaḻaṅkum camaṇat tiruvuruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. it
   tālukā cempāṭṭūril tīrttaṅkarariṉ uruvam kāṇappaṭuvatuṭaṉ ciṅkat
   tūṇkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṅkattūṇkaḷ pallavar kālattil pallava
   aracarkaḷ kaṭṭiya kōyilkaḷil kāṇappaṭuvatāl, iṅkup paṇṭaikkālattil
   pallava aracarāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kōyil iruntirukkavēṇṭum. ittālukā
   tirukkaṭṭaḷai cuntarēsvarar kōyil cācaṉam kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉuṭaiya 10
   āvatu āṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭatu. itil vaḷattāmaṅkalam paḷḷiccey
   kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.[157] (cey = vayal).

   tirumayyam tālukāvil poṉṉamarāpati kōḷīcuvarar kōvil cācaṉam ‘ollaiyūr
   kūṟṟattuk koṉṟaiyūrāṉa uttama cōḻapurattup poṉṉamarāpati paḷḷic cantam,
   kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.[158] it tālukā kāraiyūr cuntararācap perumāḷ kōyil
   cācaṉam, ‘ollaiyūr kūṟṟattuk kāraiyūr paḷḷic canta nilattai’k
   kūṟukiṟatu. [159] pulālaik kuṭiyil, pāṟaiyil amaikkappaṭṭa ciṟu camaṇak
   kōyil uṇṭu. tēvar malai eṉṉum iṭamum camaṇak kōyilē.
   kuḷattūr tālukā kuṉṉāṇṭār kōyil eṉṉum kukaikkōyil camaṇak kōyilākum.
   ittālukā aṉṉavācal paḷḷi ūruṇikku mēṟkil uḷḷa teṉṉan tōppil iraṇṭu
   camaṇat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. it tālukā vīrakkuṭikku arukil uḷḷa
   camaṇarmēṭu eṉṉum iṭattil camaṇat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ pūmiyiliruntu
   kiṭaittaṉa. kuḷattūrt tālukā tiruppūril oru camaṇa uruvam kiṭaittatu.
   ṣe tālukā tēkkāṭṭūril camaṇat tiru uruvam uṇṭu. ṣe kaṇṇaṅ kuṭiyil oru
   camaṇat tirumēṉi kiṭaittuḷḷatu.[160] ṣe virālūril camaṇa uruvam uḷatu.
   kīḻaittaṉiyam eṉṉum ūril cila camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷ cila āṇṭu kaḷukku
   muṉṉark kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivaiyellām, paṇṭaik kālattil ivviṭaṅkaḷil
   camaṇar iruntatai uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. ammaṇaṅkuṟicci eṉṉum ūr iṅku
   uṇṭu. ippeyar iṅku amaṇar iruntaṉar eṉpataik kāṭṭukiṟatu.

                        7. tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam

   tiruvārūr: ivvūril paṇṭaik kālattil camaṇar celvākkup peṟṟiruntaṉar.
   akkālattil ivvūrttirukkuḷam mikac ciṟiyatāka iruntatu. acciṟu kuḷattaic
   cūḻntu camaṇarkaḷiṉ paḷḷikaḷum, maṭaṅkaḷum, nilaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. ki.pi.
   7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉṉar (appar campantar kālattukkuc caṟṟu muṉṉar)
   ivvūril caivac camaṇar kalakam uṇṭāki iṅkirunta camaṇarkaḷaic caivar
   turattiṉar. taṇṭi aṭikaḷ, naminantiyaṭikaḷ eṉṉum caivanāyaṉmārkaḷ
   kālattil ikkalakam nikaḻntatākap periya purāṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.[161]
   ikkalakattiṉ payaṉāka icciṟu kuḷattaic cūḻntirunta camaṇarkaḷiṉ
   kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum nilaṅkaḷum iṭittup paṟikkappaṭṭup periya kuḷamākat
   tōṇṭappaṭṭatu. ippōtu ikkuḷam patiṉeṭṭu ēkkar uḷḷa periya iṭapparappaik
   koṇṭuḷḷatu. ik kuḷattiṉ perumpakuti paṇṭaik kālattil camaṇariṉ nilamāka
   iruntatu eṉpatu aṟiyat takkatu.
   centalai: tañcait tālukāvil uḷḷa ivvūr cantiralēkai eṉṟu paṇṭaik
   kālattil pēr peṟṟiruntatu. iṅkuḷḷa cuntarēccarar eṉṉum civaṉ kōyil
   veḷik kōpura vāyiliṉ iṭatupuṟac cuvaril uḷḷa cācaṉattāl iṅkup
   paṇṭaikkālattil camaṇar irunta ceyti aṟiyappaṭum. ac cācaṉap pakuti
   itu: ‘kōppara kēcari paṉmarkku yāṇṭu 12-vatu
   kā...................ṟkuṭip paḷḷiyuṭaiya ārampa vīraṉēṉ kaiyeḻuttu.
   vaṭakavira................. paḷḷiyuṭaiya kaṉakacēṉapaṭārar kaiyāl yāṉ
   koṇṭu kaṭava.’[162] maṟṟoru cācaṉam, ‘naṅkai oḷi mātiyār tāyār nakka
   nīli’ [163] eṉpavar poṉ tāṉam ceytataik kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivvūril ippōtu
   iṭintu kiṭakkum oru kōyiliṉ kaṟṟūṇ oṉṟil camaṇa tīrttaṅkarariṉ ciṟiya
   tiruvuruvam iruntataip pārttatāka eṉ naṇpar oruvar kūṟukiṉṟār.
   tiruttuṟaippūṇṭi: tiruttuṟaippūṇṭit tālukāvil uḷḷatu. ivvūr
   maruntīccurar kōyiliṉ maṇṭapattil uḷḷa, tiripuvaṉa cakkaravartti
   irācarāca tēvar 3-uṭaiya II-āvatu āṇṭil (ki.pi. 1227. mē. 15).
   eḻutappaṭṭa cācaṉattil, cāttamaṅkalattaic cērnta ‘paḷḷiccantam’.
   kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.[164] itaṉāl, ivvūrukkarukil irunta cāttamaṅkalattil
   camaṇak kōyiluk kurittāṉa nilaṅkaḷ irunta ceyti aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu.
   ātalāl, paṇṭaikkālattil iṅkuc camaṇar iruntirukkavēṇṭum.
   tirunākēccuram: kumpakōṇam tālukāvaic cērnta tirunākēccurar kōyiliṉ
   maṇṭapak kaṟṟūṇil uḷḷa cācaṉam. ‘teṉkarait tiraimūr nāṭṭil,’ irunta
   ‘milāṭuṭaiyār paḷḷi,’ eṉṉum camaṇak kōyilaik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. inta
   milāṭuṭaiyār paḷḷi, tirukkōvalūril irunta milāṭa aracaṉāl kaṭṭap
   paṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. muṟkālattil iṅku oru camaṇak kōyil iṭintu
   kiṭantateṉṟum, ak kōyiṟ kaṟkaḷaik koṇṭu ippōtuḷḷa tirunākēccurattuc
   caivakkōyil kaṭṭappaṭṭa teṉṟum ivvūrār kūṟuvar. ammaṉ kōyil maṇṭapat
   tūṇkaḷil ippōtum camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai, ivvūrār
   kūṟuvatai uṟutip paṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. ivvūrukku arukil uḷḷa vayalkaḷil
   camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.[165] tirunākēccurattiṟkup
   paṇṭaikkālattil ‘kumāra mārttāṇṭa puram,’ eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkiyateṉṟum,
   iṅkirunta milāṭuṭaiyār paḷḷiyil maṇṭapattaiyum kōpurattaiyum oru
   vaṇikar kaṭṭiṉār eṉṟum irājakēcari varmaṉ eṉṉum cōḻaṉatu 22 āvatu āṇṭil
   eḻutappaṭṭa cācaṉam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   ---------
   [161]. intak kalakattaip paṟṟi innūl, ‘camaṇa camayam kuṉṟiya varalāṟu’
   eṉṉum atikārattiṟ kāṇka.
   [162]. Top. Ins. Vol ii (1293) S.I.I. (Toxtf) Vol VI NO.443
   [163]. S.I.I. (Texts) Vol VI. NO. 422
   [164]. Top. Ins Vol II (1527. 466 of 1912)
   [165]. S.I.I. Vol III (No.91) M.E.R. 1912, p 7 and 62 S.I.I. Vol II P.
   116
   ---------
   tiruppukalūr (varttamāṉīccuram): ivvūr naṉṉilam irayil nilaiyattukkuk
   kiḻakkē nāṉku mailil uḷḷatu. iṅku vartta māṉīccurar kōyil uṇṭu. ik
   kōyil ippōtu caivak kōyilāka uḷḷatu. āṉāl, ikkōyiliṉ peyaraikkoṇṭa itu
   paṇṭaik kālattil camaṇak kōyilāka iruntatu eṉpatai aṟiyalām.
   srīvarttamāṉar (makā vīrar) irupattu nāṉkāvatu tīrttaṅkararāvar. ic
   camaṇak kōyil ki.pi 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉṉarē caivarāl kaippaṟṟappaṭṭuc
   caivak kōyilākkap paṭṭatu. apparum campantarum ikkōyilaip pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar.
   iṅkuc camaṇar paṇṭaik kālattil iruntaṉar.
   paḻaiyāṟai: itaṉaip ‘paḻaiyāṟu,’ ‘paḻacai’, eṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar.
   paṭṭīccurattukkut teṉ kiḻakkē oru mailil uḷḷatu. cōḻa aracarkaḷiṉ
   uṟaviṉar iṅku vāḻntiruntaṉar. iṅkup paṇṭaik kālattil camaṇar iruntaṉar.
   ki.pi. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, appar cuvāmi kālattil, iṅkē kalakam ēṟpaṭṭuc
   camaṇar turattappaṭṭa ceytiyaip periya purāṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.[166, 167]
   ki.pi. 11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum iṅkuc camaṇarum camaṇak kōyilum irunta ceyti
   aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. iṅkirunta camaṇak kōyilil eḻuntaruḷiyirunta
   arukakkaṭavuḷmītu iyaṟṟappaṭṭa iraṇṭu ceyyuḷkaḷ yāpparuṅkala virutti
   uraiyil mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅku vāḻntirunta cōḻa aracaṉ ik
   kōyilukkuc ciṟappuc ceytāṉ eṉpatum viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. ac ceyyuḷ varumāṟu:
   ‘tāḻi yōṅku malark kaṇṇavar taṇṇaṭi
       pāḻi yōṅku puṉalār paḻai yāṟṟuḷ
       kāḻi niṉṟam matiyāṉ maticērntu
       vāḻi eṉṟu vaṇaṅka viṉai cērā.’
       ‘muḻaṅku kaḷiyāṉai mūrik kaṭaṟpaṭai muṟitār maṉṉar
       vaḻaṅku miṭamellān taṉpukaḻē pōkkiya vaivēl viṇṇaṉ
       ceḻun taṇpūm paḻacaiyuṭ ciṟantatu nāḷuñ ceya
       veḻunta cētikat tuḷḷirunta vaṇṇalaṭi
       viḻuntaṇpū malarkaḷāl viyantu nāḷun toḻat
       toṭarntu niṉṟa valviṉai tuṟantupō mālarō’

   maruttuvakkuṭi: ivvūr, pāpanācam tālukāvil uḷḷatu. ivvūr airāvatīsvarar
   kōyil maṇṭapattil uḷḷa, tiripuvaṉa cakkaravartti srī kulōttuṅka cōḻa
   tēvar 3 uṭaiya 16 āvatu āṇṭil (ki.pi. 1194 il) eḻutappaṭṭa cācaṉattil,
   jaṉanātapuram eṉṉum ūril irunta cētikula māṇikkap perumpaḷḷi, kaṅkaruḷa
   cuntarapperumpaḷḷi eṉṉum iraṇṭu camaṇak kōyilkaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.[168]
   itaṉāl, ivvūrukkarukil camaṇarum camaṇak kōyilkaḷum irunta ceyti
   aṟiyappaṭum.
   tiruvalañcuḻi: itu kumpakōṇam tālukāviṉul uḷḷatu. iṅkuḷḷa civaṉ
   kōyilukkarukil cila camaṇa uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.[169] itaṉāl,
   ivvūril paṇṭaikkālattil camaṇar iruntatu aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu.
   maṉṉārkuṭi: maṉṉārkuṭit tālukāviṉ talainakar. paṇṭaik kālattil ivvūril
   camaṇar atikamāka iruntaṉar. ippōtum cila camaṇar uḷḷaṉar. oru camaṇak
   kōyilum irukkiṟatu.[170] iṅkuḷḷa rājakōpāla cuvāmi kōyil tuvajastampam,
   jaiṉaruṭaiya māṉastampam pōṉṟirukkiṟapaṭiyāl iḵtu ātiyil camaṇak
   kōyilāka iruntirukkakkūṭum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar.[171]
   tīpaṅkuṭi: naṉṉilam tālukāvil uḷḷatu. naṉṉilattiṟkut teṉmēṟku 7 mailil
   uḷḷatu. ituvum paḻaiya camaṇa ūr. iṅkirunta jayaṅkoṇṭār eṉṉum camaṇar
   ‘tīpaṅkuṭip pattu,’ eṉṉum ciṟanta, iṉiya, aḻakiya pāṭalkaḷaip
   pāṭiyuḷḷār. ivarē ‘kaliṅkattup paraṇi,’ eṉṉum nūlai iyaṟṟiyatākak
   kūṟuvar. it tīpaṅkuṭiyil ippōtum camaṇar uḷḷaṉar. camaṇak kōyil oṉṟum
   irukkiṟatu.[172]
   tañcāvūr māvaṭṭattil iṉṉum pala iṭaṅkaḷil camaṇar irunta ceyti
   cācaṉaṅkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. ‘arumoḻi tēva vaḷanāṭṭu iṅkaḷanāṭṭup
   pālaiyūrp paḷḷi,’[173] ‘aricilukkum kāvirikkum naṭuvāṉa uyyak koṇṭa
   vaḷanāṭṭut tiraimūrnāṭṭup paḷḷiccantam,’[174] ‘tiruvāli nāṭṭuk
   kuṟuvāṇiyakkuṭi paḷḷi,’[175] ‘uyyak koṇṭa vaḷanāṭṭu amaṇkuṭi’[176] eṉa
   varum cācaṉap pakutikaḷāl tañcāvūr māvaṭṭattil camaṇar irunta ceyti
   aṟiyappaṭum.
   amaṇkuṭi: ‘cōḻa maṇṭalattu uyyak koṇṭa vaḷanāṭṭai’c cērnta veṇṇāṭil
   amaṇkuṭi eṉṉum ūr iruntateṉṟum ivvūr piṟkālattil kēraḷāntakac caturvēti
   maṅkalam eṉṟu peyar māṟṟappaṭṭateṉṟum srī irācarāca cōḻaratu
   cēṉāpatiyāṉa kiruṣṇaṉ irāmaṉāṉa mummuṭi cōḻa pirammarāyaṉ eṉpavar
   ivvūril vāḻntiruntār eṉṟum, tañcai irāca rācēccurak kōyil kalveṭ
   ṭeḻuttukkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. [177] ivvūrp peyarē iṅkuc camaṇar
   vāḻntiruntaṉar eṉpatait terivikkiṟatu.
   karuntiṭṭaik kuṭi: (karuntaṭṭāṉkuṭi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar) aṅkuc camaṇar
   muṉṉāḷil ciṟappuṟṟiruntaṉar. ippōtum iṅkuc camaṇar uḷḷaṉar. camaṇa
   ālayamum uṇṭu.
   kukūr: iṅkuk kulōttuṅkaṉ I kālattil kulōttuṅkaṉ peyarāl perum paḷḷi
   kaṭṭappaṭṭatu.[178]

   kāṇka.

                      8. irāmanātapura māvaṭṭam

   kōvilaṅkuḷam: aṟuppukkōṭṭait tālukāvil uḷḷa ivvūril ampalappacāmi kōyil
   mēṭaiyiṉ mēṟku, teṟkup pakkattil cila cācaṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   ippōtu, inta mēṭaimaṭṭum uḷḷatu; kōyil illai. iṅkuḷḷa cācaṉam nalla
   ilakkiya naṭaiyuḷḷatu. tiripuvaṉa cakkaravartti srī kulōttuṅka cōḻa
   tēvaratu 48 āvatu āṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa intac cācaṉam, mukkuṭainātarukku
   (arukakkaṭavuḷukku) poṉmayamāṉa maṇṭapamum vimāṉamum, mukkuṭai nātar,
   iyakki ivarkaḷiṉ cepput tirumēṉikaḷum, taṇṇīrppantalum, ikkōyil kaṭṭa
   nilamum ivvūril irunta camaṇarkaḷāl amaikkappaṭṭa ceytiyaik kūṟukiṟatu.
   iccācaṉattil ivvūr ‘vēmpu vaṭanāṭṭuc ceṅkāṭṭirukkaiyaic cērnta
   kumpaṉūr’ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. iṅkuḷḷa iṉṉoru cācaṉam,
   ikkumpaṉūraiyum, kuranti, kuṉṟattūr, puttūr eṉṉum ūrkaḷaiyum aracāṇṭa
   ciṟṟaracaṉ cōḻakōṉ eṉpavaṉaiyum pukaḻntu kūṟukiṟatu.[179]
   iruppaikkuṭi: cāttūrt tālukāvil uḷḷa erukkaṅkuṭikku araikkāl mailil
   uḷḷa ērikku mēṟkil oru pāṟaiyil uḷḷa vaṭṭeḻuttuc cācaṉam uḷḷatu.
   iccācaṉam pāṇṭiyaṉ caṭaiyaṉ māṟaṉ srīvallapaṉatu 18 āvatu āṇṭil
   eḻutappaṭṭatu. ivvaracaṉukkuk kīḻppaṭṭirunta ‘iruppaikkuṭi kiḻavaṉ’
   eṉpavaṉ ‘periyapaḷḷi’yaik kaṭṭip ‘pāḻikkuḷam’ eṉṉum ēriyaip
   paḻutupārtta ceytikaḷ itil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iruppaikkuṭi kiḻavaṉukku
   ‘eṭṭicāttaṉ’ eṉṉum peyarum iruntatākat terikiṟatu. ivaṉ camaṇaṉāka
   irukkavēṇṭum ivaṉ kaṭṭiya ‘periya paḷḷi’yum camaṇak kōyil eṉpatil
   aiyamillai.[180]
   pirāṉmalai: tiruppattūrt tālukā pirāṉmalaiyil uḷḷa maṅkainātar kōyilil
   cācaṉaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. iccācaṉam. ‘kaṭalaṭaiyātilaṅkai koṇṭa cōḻavaḷa nāṭṭu
   teṉkō nāṭṭu iṭaiyāṟṟūr’ paḷḷiccanta nilaṅkaḷaik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.[181]
   iṅkuḷḷa maṟṟoru cācaṉam, ‘kūṭalūrāṉa aiññūṟṟuva maṅkalattup paḷḷiccanta
   nilaṅkaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟatu.[182] paḷḷiccantam eṉpatu camaṇap paḷḷikkuriya
   nilaṅkaḷākum.
   iḷaiyāṉ kuṭi: paramakkuṭi irayil nilaiyattukku 7 mailil uḷḷatu ivvūr.
   (ivvūril caiva aṭiyārāṉa iḷaiyāṉkuṭi māṟa nāyaṉār iruntār.) ivvūr civaṉ
   kōyilukku veḷiyē camaṇat tiruvuruvam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉai
   ‘amaṇacāmi’ (camaṇak kaṭavuḷ) eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. ivvūrār veḷḷikkiḻamai
   tōṟum inta amaṇacāmiyaip pūcittu vaṇaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl,
   muṟkālattil iṅkuc camaṇar iruntirukkavēṇṭum eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu.
   mañcaputtūrc ceṭṭimārkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. ivvūr ērikkaraiyil oru
   camaṇa uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.[183]
   paḷḷi maṭam: aṟuppukkōṭṭait tālukāvil uḷḷa ik kirāmam paṇṭaik kālattil
   camaṇar kirāmamāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉat terikiṟatu. ikkirāmattiṉ
   paṇṭaip peyar, ‘tirupparuttikkuṭi nāṭṭut tiruccuḻiyil paḷḷi maṭai’
   eṉpatu. iṅkuḷḷa kalānātacuvāmikōyil cācaṉam oṉṟil, vēmpu nāṭṭuk kuratti
   tirukkāṭṭampaḷḷi tēvar eṉṉum camaṇak kōyilil nantā viḷakkukkākac
   cāttaṉkāri eṉpavar aimpatu āṭukaḷait tāṉam ceyta ceyti
   kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.[184]
   aṉumantakuṭi: irāmanātapurattukku vaṭakkē 371/2 mailil uḷḷatu.
   tiruvāṭāṉait tālukāvil uḷḷa ikkirāmattil maḻavanāta cuvāmi kōyil eṉṉum
   camaṇak kōyil uṇṭu. ik kōyiliṉ etiril uḷḷa uṭaintu pōṉa cācaṉam cakam
   1455 (ki.pi 1535) il vijayanakara aracaṉ (peyar kāṇappaṭavillai)
   kālattil eḻutappaṭṭatu. itil, ‘muttūṟṟuk kūṟṟattu añcukōṭṭai’ eṉṉum
   ūrum, ṣa muttūṟṟuk kūṟṟattu ‘kuruvaṭimiṭi...........eṉṉum
   jiṉēntiramaṅkalam’ eṉṉum ūrum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. jiṉēntiramaṅkalam
   eṉṉum peyar iṅkuc camaṇar iruntaṉar eṉpatai viḷakkukiṉṟatu.[185]
   ippōtum camaṇakkōyil uḷatu.[186] iyakki kōmaṭēcuvarar mutaliya nāṉku
   cempuvikkirakaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉavām.
   tirukkaḷākkuṭi/: tiruppattūrt tālukāvil uḷḷa ivvūr, tiruppattūrukkup
   patiṉēḻu mail cēymaiyil uḷḷatu. iṅku malaiyum kōyilum uṇṭu. ikkōyil
   paṇṭaikkālattil ārukatak kōyilāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum. ippōtu caivak
   kōyilāka uḷḷatu. ikkōyilil, pārcuvanāta cuvāmiyiṉ tirumēṉi oṉṟu
   vīṟṟiruppatupōṉṟu amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ittiru mēṉiyiṉ talaikkumēl
   aintalai nākamum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.[187]
   inta māvaṭṭattil iraṇiyūr, iḷaiyāttaṅkuṭi, nācciyāpurattukku oru mailil
   uḷḷa naṭuvikōṭṭai eṉṉum ūrkaḷil paṇṭaikkālattil camaṇar iruntaṉar
   eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ uḷa.[188]
   periyapaṭṭiṉam: irāmanātapuram irayil nilaittiliruntu teṉkiḻakkākap
   pattu mailil uḷḷa kaṭaṟkaraik kirāmam. iṅkuc camaṇa uruvaccilaikaḷ
   kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.[189]
   tēvipaṭṭiṉam: irāmanātapuram tālukāvil uḷḷatu. ivvūril uḷḷa
   tilakēcuvarar kōvil cācaṉam, ‘‘iṭaikkuḷ nāṭṭuc ceḻuvaṉūrāṉa catturu
   payaṅkara nallūrum, kiṭāramāṉa kiṭāraṅ koṇṭa cōḻapuramum, koḻuvūr
   nāṭṭuk kiḷiyūrum ākiya ivvūr nāṉkellaik kuṭpaṭṭa nilattil’’ irunta
   paḷḷic cantattaik kūṟukiṟatu.[190] itaṉāl iṅkē camaṇar iruntaṉar eṉpatu
   terikiṟatu.
   kiṭāram: irāmanāta purattukkut teṉmēṟkē 14 mailil uḷḷatu. intak
   kirāmattiṉ teṟkē oru camaṇa uruvam irukkiṟatu.[191]
   kōvilkuḷam: irāmanātapurattukkut teṉmēṟkil 34 mailil uḷḷatu. iṅku
   iraṇṭu camaṇat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.[192]
   kulacēkara nallūr: (nallūr) tiruccūḷai eṉṉum iṭattiliruntu mēṟkē 8
   mailil uḷḷatu. irāmanātapurattiliruntu vaṭamēṟkē 50 mailil uḷḷatu. iṅku
   iṭintupōṉa oru civaṉ kōyil uṇṭu. intak kōyil muṉpu camaṇak kōyilāka
   iruntateṉṟu kūṟuppaṭukiṟatu. intac kirāmattil camaṇar iruntaṉar eṉṟum
   kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷait turattiviṭṭu ikkōyilaic caivak kōyilākac
   ceytāṉ eṉṟum ūrār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.[193]
   mañciyūr: irāmanāta purattiliruntu vaṭamēṟkē 15 mailil uḷḷatu. ik
   kirāmattiṉ mēṟkē oru parlāṅkil oru camaṇat tiruvuruvam irukkiṟatu.[194]
   celuvaṉūr: celvanallūr eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. mutukuḷattūrukkut teṉkiḻakkil
   91/2 mailil uḷḷatu. irāmanātapurattiṟkut teṉmēṟkē 23 mailil uḷḷatu.
   ikkirāmattiṟku mēṟkē kuṭikaḷ aṟṟa oru kirāmattil oru camaṇat tirumēṉi
   irukkiṟatu.[195]
